Conclusion: 结构自动机在解码阶段最有效，APD能以不回溯token的方式保证可接受输出并在紧长度限制下接近rollback；后处理过滤在数据充足时与APD接近；训练阶段显式惩罚被拒token收益有限，因交叉熵本身会快速降低其概率。

Abstract: Neural semantic parsers generate meaning representations token by token, yet well-formedness depends on references, scope, and graph relations that unfold during generation. Constrained decoding prevents locally invalid tokens but does not guarantee that a valid prefix can be completed within a fixed output-length limit. We investigate structural automata at three stages of DRS parsing (decoding, post hoc processing, and training) and additionally evaluate decoding on AMR. For decoding, we introduce accepting-path decoding (APD), which maintains a verified completion within the remaining length limit and follows the model's preferred token whenever that token can also be completed. APD guarantees an accepted output without retracting tokens. Under tight length limits, it improves Smatch++ over local masking by 5.1 and 2.4 points on DRS and by 6.4 and 3.2 points on AMR, comparable to rollback under the same limit, which retracts tokens. For post hoc processing, invalid outputs concentrate on the same inputs: eight unconstrained samples yield no valid output for 10.2% of inputs with limited training data, compared with 0.4% with full data, where filtering comes within 0.2 Smatch++ points of APD. For training, penalizing automaton-rejected tokens improves development Smatch++ by 3.6-11.0 points after 10% of training, but this advantage diminishes as cross-entropy alone reduces the probability assigned to rejected tokens from 14% to below 2% within the first sixth of training with limited data.

</details>


### [71] [When VLMs Destroy What They Know: Spatial Representations Fall Below Lexical Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13910)
*Shubh Garg,Chen Shani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出度量指标 EICS 与基准 CASP-VL，检验视觉语言模型内部表征对概念的编码稳定性，发现空间关系的编码忠实度显著低于词汇信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs 越来越多地用于图像分析与推理，但其内部表征是否忠实编码推理所需的结构化关系尚不清楚；同时，基于输出的评测可能会掩盖表征层面的系统性缺陷。

Method: 提出 EICS（Evaluation-Invariant Concept Stability）指标，通过在受控的单概念干预下测试表征是否一致地编码概念；并构建 CASP-VL 基准，该基准由 Visual Genome 场景图生成、经过因果控制的空间扰动样本组成；在多种 VLM 架构上进行评估分析。

Result: 空间关系的表征忠实度显著低于词汇信息，即使这些模型在组合型基准上的得分明显高于随机水平。进一步分析显示，关系信息在谓词自身的 token 上可被识别，但当句子被聚合为全局表征时（无论经由 EOS token 还是均值池化）该信息即丢失。

Conclusion: 存在一种输出评测无法察觉的系统性失败：VLM 并未在下游所使用的表征中保留其自身输入里已包含的关系信息。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) increasingly analyze and reason about images, yet it remains unclear whether their internal representations faithfully encode the structured relations required for such reasoning. We introduce EICS (Evaluation-Invariant Concept Stability), a metric for testing whether representations consistently encode concepts under controlled one-concept interventions, and CASP-VL (Causally controlled Spatial Perturbations for Vision-Language models), a benchmark of causally controlled spatial perturbations derived from Visual Genome scene graphs. Across multiple VLM architectures, we find that spatial relations are represented substantially less faithfully than lexical information, even when the same models score well above chance on compositional benchmarks. Further analysis shows that the relation is identifiable at the predicate's own tokens but is lost when the sentence is aggregated into a global representation, whether through the (EOS) token or by mean pooling. These findings reveal a systematic failure invisible to output-based evaluation: VLMs do not preserve, in the representation used downstream, relational information that is present in their own input.

</details>


### [72] [RSI-Anything: Unlocking Recursive Self-Improvement with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13912)
*Taoyong Cui,Pinxian Zeng,Wanghan Xu,Hao Xiao,Shuhan Xue,Qiang Gao,Wanli Ouyang,Pheng Ann Heng,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出领域通用框架 RSI-Anything，基于 JEPA-Anything 用预测世界模型引导动作与学习数据选择，实现递归自我改进，在多领域显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）旨在将交互经验转化为持久改进，但外部反馈成本高，且随策略演化，世界模型预测可能变得不可靠。

Method: 构建于 JEPA-Anything 之上的领域通用框架 RSI-Anything：用预测世界模型引导动作选择与学习数据选择；观测反馈驱动策略/工作流改进与条件世界模型自适应；通过组件与兼容性检查控制更新后的策略-模型对的保留。

Result: 代码修复中（编码器固定），成功率从静态验证器 26.3% 提升至预测选择 29.2%、世界模型自适应后 31.2%，测试用量相当；GUI 评估任务完成度更高，平均动作减少 39.4%；连续五个策略版本下实验室规划分数从 21.80% 提升至 46.01%，翻倍以上；反应优化在相同 40 次实验预算下平均最佳观测产率达 96.15%，优于贝叶斯优化的 82.16%；端到端免疫科学应用进一步连接工作流演化与预测建模以生成治疗假设。

Conclusion: 结果表明，预测世界模型与保留的系统更新能够在多样环境中实现递归改进。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) aims to transform interaction experience into persistent improvements, yet external feedback is costly and world-model predictions may become unreliable as policies evolve. We introduce RSI-Anything, a domain-general framework built on JEPA-Anything that uses predictive world models to guide action and learning-data selection. Observed feedback drives policy or workflow improvement and conditional world-model adaptation, while component and compatibility checks govern the retention of updated policy-model pairs. Across diverse computational and scientific domains, RSI-Anything demonstrates substantial gains. In code repair, with the coder fixed, success increases from 26.3% with a static verifier to 29.2% through predictive selection and 31.2% after world-model adaptation, at comparable test usage. Graphical user interface (GUI) evaluations showed higher task completion, while average actions decrease by 39.4%. Across five successive policy versions, laboratory-planning scores more than double from 21.80% to 46.01%. In reaction optimization, RSI-Anything achieves 96.15% mean best observed yield versus 82.16% for Bayesian optimization under the same 40-experiment budget. An end-to-end immune-science application further connects workflow evolution and predictive modeling with therapeutic hypothesis generation. These results demonstrate how predictive world models and retained system updates enable recursive improvement across diverse environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [73] [Faithfulness Without Anchors: A Position Paper on the Structural Misalignments of Explainable AI Faithfulness Metrics for Feature Importance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12493)
*Marco Zullich,Emily Schiller,Ivan Gentile,David Dembinsky,Adriano Lucieri,Kilian Göller,Steffen Seitz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，指出可解释AI（XAI）在提升信任方面总体失败，主要因其解释常与模型不一致（忠实性不足或失真）。现有忠实性度量存在显著局限，使量化不可行或计算昂贵。作者归纳当前度量方法的五大缺陷，并提出四条研究路径以提升其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XAI工具常被宣传为通过提升算法的人类可理解性来增强对机器学习模型的信任，但近十年证据表明这一目标总体失败，反而助长对错误预测的过度依赖并进而引发算法厌恶。XAI目前主要作为调试工具提供零散洞见。核心问题之一是XAI方法过度简化模型内部机制，导致解释与模型不一致（不忠实/失真）。严格计算忠实性本可量化解释可信度的一个要素，但现有度量存在明显局限，导致量化不可行或计算昂贵，因此亟需改进。

Method: 立场论文（position paper）。通过梳理与批判现有忠实性度量，总结其在可靠性方面的问题，并提出改进研究方向。作者归纳现有度量方法的五大主要缺陷，并提出四条提升其可靠性的研究路径。

Result: 论文的核心贡献是识别并归纳当前忠实性度量的五大主要缺陷，指出这些缺陷使得忠实性的量化要么不可行，要么计算代价高昂；同时提出四条可能的研究方向以增强度量的可靠性。

Conclusion: XAI以提升信任为主要目标的实践总体未能奏效，反而可能引发过度依赖与算法厌恶；其更现实的定位是调试工具。忠实性（解释与模型的近似程度）是可信度的重要组成，但现有度量局限重重。作者主张通过改进度量方法来支撑对解释忠实性的严格量化，并给出四个研究方向。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) tools have often been presented as methods to increase trust in machine learning models by making aspects of their algorithms more human-understandable. However, evidence accumulated over the last decade points to an overall failure of this objective, instead promoting overreliance on incorrect predictions, which can subsequently lead to algorithmic aversion. Rather, XAI appears to function primarily as a debugging tool, useful for obtaining anecdotal insights into, for example, features learned by the model. One of the main issues underlying this limitation is that XAI methods often oversimplify the inner workings of the model, resulting in misalignment between the explanation and the model itself - a behavior referred to as unfaithfulness or infidelity. Faithfulness can be interpreted as the degree of approximation between an explanation and the model. Computing faithfulness in a rigorous manner would thus make it possible to quantify one of the various elements of trustworthiness of an explanation. However, existing metrics present significant limitations, making this quantification either infeasible or computationally expensive. In this position paper, we outline five major shortcomings of current faithfulness metrics and propose four possible avenues for research to bolster their reliability.

</details>


### [74] [EliteTable: Self-Improving Program Search for Executable Control Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12536)
*Stuart Farmer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EliteTable 是一种由大语言模型引导、以固定容量"高分解答表"驱动新提案、编辑与重组的自改进程序搜索算法，在六项 Gymnasium 控制任务上验证了搜索过程中的性能提升及部分任务的迁移增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用大语言模型引导程序搜索，并通过一个固定容量的高分解答表来组织新提案生成、现有解答编辑与多解答重组，以期在程序搜索过程中持续自我改进，并将选出的优胜解答的性能增益迁移到新的评测回合。

Method: 算法层面：维护一个固定容量的高分解答表，指导三类操作——新提案生成、既有解答编辑、多个解答的组合重构。实验层面：在六项 Gymnasium 控制任务上评测，每项任务运行十代、每代五十个候选槽位，并对每项任务进行十次独立初始化的搜索；同时考察选出的优胜解答在留出回合（held-out episodes）上的配对表现。

Result: 六项任务中，候选得分的代际中位数从第一代到最终代均上升；优胜解答的搜索得分中位数在五项任务中上升。CartPole-v1 中，十次搜索的优胜解答均在首代即达到搜索得分上限。六项任务中有四项的全部十组配对留出端点均改善。BipedalWalker-v3 与 CarRacing-v3 有改善但未达到其报告目标。Acrobot-v1 的十次搜索中有六次在留出回合上表现下降，配对变化中位数为负。

Conclusion: 结果证明了程序搜索过程中的改进，以及所选优胜解答的增益在部分任务上向新回合的迁移。仍需通过对比实验与消融实验来确定 EliteTable 的相对性能表现及其各组成部分的具体贡献。

Abstract: EliteTable is a self-improving program-search algorithm guided by large language models (LLMs) that uses a fixed-capacity table of high-scoring solutions to guide new proposals, edits of existing solutions, and remixes of several solutions. Performance was evaluated across six Gymnasium control tasks, each with ten generations and fifty candidate slots per generation. Each task was evaluated across ten independently initialized searches. Candidate-score medians rose between the first and final generations in all six tasks, and selected-winner search-score medians rose in five. In CartPole-v1, the selected winner reached the search-score ceiling in the first generation of all ten searches. All ten paired held-out endpoints improved in four of the six tasks. BipedalWalker-v3 and CarRacing-v3 improved without reaching their reporting targets. Six of ten Acrobot-v1 searches declined on held-out episodes, and the median paired change was negative. The results document improvement during program search and the transfer of selected-winner gains to new episodes in some tasks. Comparisons and ablations are needed to establish EliteTable's relative performance and the contributions of its components.

</details>


### [75] [Equal-Budget Monte Carlo Tree Search versus Randomized-Restart List Scheduling for DAG Workflows: A Controlled Equal-Budget Computational Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12542)
*Yuyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在预算受控的对照实验中，MCTS 调度 DAG 工作流的表现劣于同等搜索预算的随机重启列表调度器（主预算下差 2.88%），原因是其选择规则确定性地锁定首个展开子节点；替换为教科书式 UCT 可消除大部分差距但无法反转符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCTS 常被提议用于 DAG 工作流调度，但多数研究从未运行能证伪该主张的对照实验——即给更简单方法相同的搜索努力。作者旨在以公平预算的受控协议补齐这一缺失的比较。

Method: 采用预注册、分析计划锁定于数据收集前的对照协议。所有实验臂获得相同预算（相同数量的候选调度构建与评估）：主臂为 MCTS 调度方法，对照臂为无搜索树的随机重启列表调度器，后续补充臂为教科书式 UCT MCTS。共 768 个确认阶段调度由独立检查者验证有效且完工时间正确；参数域为 N∈{8,16,32} 任务、2–8 处理器、预算至 2048，并另设独立探索池以检验预算饥饿假说。

Result: 主预算 2048 个候选调度时，MCTS 比重启法差 2.88%（95% CI [-3.62%,-2.15%]；96 个留出实例中 72 个存在正相对提升空间）。差距并非预算不足：探索池中重启法自提升 1.85%，而 MCTS 仅 0.24%；MCTS 首次结果有 700/720 仅为其被赋予的热启动。结构性映射显示：稀疏不规则工作流上重启法胜出，规则分层工作流上无差异，密集工作流无提升空间。改用教科书 UCT 消除大部分差距（-0.26%，95% CI [-0.71%,+0.23%]），但符号未反转。

Conclusion: 在受控合成域内，MCTS 相对简单随机重启列表调度器无优势，缺陷源于选择规则确定性锁定与试验相关性而非预算饥饿；就其因果归因于完工时间仍属推断。结论限于所研究的小规模合成域与至 2048 的预算，不对更大预算作声明。

Abstract: Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) is often proposed for scheduling directed-acyclic-graph (DAG) workflows, yet most studies never run the comparison that could refute it - giving a much simpler method the same search effort. We run that comparison under a controlled protocol. All arms receive an identical budget - the same number of candidate schedules built and evaluated: an MCTS scheduling method, preregistered as the main arm; a randomized-restart list scheduler with no search tree as the control; and a textbook-UCT MCTS arm as a registered follow-up on the same domain. The analysis plan was locked before data collection; an independent checker confirmed all 768 confirmation-stage schedules valid with correct finish times. At the main budget of 2048 evaluated schedules, MCTS is 2.88% worse than the restarts (95% CI [-3.62%,-2.15%]; the 72 of 96 held-out instances with positive baseline-relative headroom). The deficit is not budget starvation: on the separate exploration pool restarts self-improve by 1.85% where MCTS gains 0.24%, and 700 of 720 of MCTS's first results are merely the warm start it was handed. The studied selection rule locks deterministically onto the first-expanded child, keeping its trials correlated while restarts draw gains from the tails; the causal link to makespan remains an inference. A structural map locates where the tree stops paying: restarts win on sparse irregular workflows, no difference is detectable on regular layered ones, and dense ones leave no room to improve. Swapping the rule for textbook UCT removes most of the deficit (-0.26%, 95% CI [-0.71%,+0.23%]) without reversing its sign. Results are confined to a controlled synthetic domain (N in {8,16,32} tasks, 2-8 processors, budgets up to 2048); we make no claim about larger budgets.

</details>


### [76] [Locate, Judge, Read: Role-Specialized Tool Agents over a Knowledge-Point Index for Open-Domain Multimodal Document QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13128)
*Wenlin Lin,Fanzhe Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowForge 将检索增强问答拆分为"搜索"与"阅读"两类专职智能体，通过知识点索引、仅搜索的 Locator、强制二次跳转的桥接判定、类型化证据池、仅阅读的 Deep Reader 与格式校验合成器组成流水线，在 M3DocVQA、MultiHop-RAG、MMLongBench-Doc 上均取得优于现有系统的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模异构文档集合上的问答需要先定位正确段落、再阅读理解。当前检索增强智能体把两类工具交给同一模型并让其自行调度，导致模型倾向于持续检索而非转入阅读，第二跳检索缺失，工具职责混杂。

Method: 按职能拆分流水线：1）混合检索的知识点索引；2）只能搜索的 Locator 智能体；3）代码调度的桥接判定器，强制补上缺失的第二跳；4）类型化、大小受限的证据池，合并每次搜索的排序结果；5）只能阅读文本、图像和表格的 Deep Reader 智能体；6）带格式校验器的合成器。使用自托管 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B（激活 3B）及 DeepSeek-V4-Flash。

Result: 在完整开放域 M3DocVQA（2,441 问、3,368 个 PDF）上 F1 达 47.5，较已发表 M3DocRAG 提升 +11.0，较同索引同模型的一次性检索答题提升 +7.1；使用 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 达 53.5。MultiHop-RAG 准确率 73.5，超过最强已发表 RAG 与 GraphRAG（71.2）。MMLongBench-Doc 上将同模型从 34.8 提升至 47.0，与 GPT-4o 阅读页面图像的 46.3 持平。逐任务消融显示 Deep Reader 在三个语料上分别贡献 3.8、9.8、0 分，与其视觉依赖程度一致；将两个智能体合并为一个并给双倍轮次会损失 1.9 F1，因为它持续搜索而不阅读；轮次预算扫描显示在四轮 Locator 处出现阶跃，之后趋于平台；查询延迟随模型调用次数而非语料规模增长。

Conclusion: 将检索与阅读按职能分离、并以代码调度强制多跳桥接，比让单一模型自主调度更有效；Deep Reader 的收益与语料的视觉依赖度正相关；延迟由模型调用数主导而非语料规模。已发布三个只读知识库基准（免费注册），并开源全部提示词与运行代码。

Abstract: Question answering over large heterogeneous document collections requires first finding the right passages and then reading them, yet current retrieval-augmented agents give one model both kinds of tools and let it schedule itself. We present KnowForge, a pipeline separated by job: a hybrid-retrieval knowledge-point index; a Locator agent that can only search; a code-scheduled bridge judge that forces the missing second hop; a typed, size-bounded evidence pool that merges every search's ranking; a Deep Reader agent that can only read text, images and tables; and a synthesizer with a format checker. With a self-hosted Qwen3.6-35B-A3B (3B active), KnowForge reaches F1 47.5 on the full open-domain M3DocVQA (2,441 questions, 3,368 PDFs), +11.0 over the published M3DocRAG system and +7.1 over one-shot retrieve-and-answer on the same index and model, and 53.5 with DeepSeek-V4-Flash. On MultiHop-RAG it reaches 73.5 accuracy, above the strongest published RAG and GraphRAG results (71.2); on the single-document MMLongBench-Doc it lifts the same model from 34.8 to 47.0, level with GPT-4o reading page images (46.3). Per-job ablations on the full benchmarks attribute the gain: the Deep Reader is worth 3.8, 9.8 and 0 points on the three corpora, tracking how visual each is; merging both agents into one with all tools and twice the rounds loses 1.9 F1 because it keeps searching instead of reading; a round-budget sweep shows a step at four Locator rounds and a plateau beyond; query latency scales with model calls rather than corpus size. We serve the three indexed benchmarks as read-only knowledge bases (free registration) and release all prompts and the runner code: https://github.com/metask-ai/knowforge-bench

</details>


### [77] [Know What You Don't Know: Agentic Data Synthesis by Evolving Generation Space for Closed-Loop Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12593)
*Wei Yang,Qing Ping,Youzhi Luo,Yawei Wang,Abhishek Kumar,Panpan Xu,Jun Huan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvoSyn——一种由学习反馈驱动的多智能体合成框架，通过"可修订状态"组织数据合成，弥合"反馈—决策鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能力提升日益依赖合成经验，而交互式智能体的有效数据合成不仅需要可执行、可验证的轨迹，还需对当前任务智能体具有学习价值。现有方法虽引入环境反馈、数据验证或学习者的失败案例，但仍存在"反馈—决策鸿沟"：观察到的弱点无法直接指明未来合成应如何调整，也无法判断这些弱点能否被当前合成空间所表示。

Method: EvoSyn是一个学习反馈驱动的多智能体框架，围绕一个可修订状态来组织合成，该状态规定哪些交互因素可被有意生成、以及合成精力应投向何处。角色专门化的多智能体系统将学习者反馈转化为合成修订；其中Designer负责分配合成方向，Planner负责将其落地为可执行经验。该流程既支持对薄弱或覆盖不足区域的空间内自适应，也支持合成空间扩展，将新发现的因子提升为显式的生成控制变量。

Result: 在τ²-Bench与CAR-Bench上，EvoSyn相比最强合成基线平均任务成功率提升13.3个百分点，同时生成质量更高、结构更广的合成经验，并发现缺少学习者反馈时会被遗漏的可控因子。

Conclusion: 通过在合成流程中引入可修订状态与角色分工的多智能体机制，EvoSyn有效弥合反馈与合成决策之间的鸿沟，实现空间内自适应与空间扩展的双重能力，显著提升交互式智能体的任务成功率与合成数据质量。

Abstract: The continued improvement of large language models increasingly depends on synthetic experience, yet effective data synthesis for interactive agents requires not only executable and verifiable trajectories, but also interactions that provide learning value for the current task agent. Existing methods increasingly incorporate environment feedback, data validation, or learner failures, but still face a feedback-to-decision gap: observed weaknesses do not directly specify how future synthesis should change, or whether they can be represented by the current synthesis space. To address this gap, we propose EvoSyn, a learning-feedback-driven multi-agent framework that organizes synthesis around a revisable state specifying what interaction factors can be deliberately generated and where synthesis effort should be allocated. A role-specialized multi-agent system translates learner feedback into synthesis revisions, while a Designer allocates synthesis directions and a Planner grounds them into executable experience. This process enables within-space adaptation toward weak or under-covered regions and synthesis-space expansion that promotes newly discovered factors into explicit generation controls. Experiments on $τ^2$-Bench and CAR-Bench show that EvoSyn improves average task success by 13.3 points over the strongest synthesis baseline, while producing higher-quality and structurally broader synthetic experience and discovering controllable factors missed without learner feedback.

</details>


### [78] [Web$hell: A Unified Interface for Agentic Web Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13416)
*Sunghwan Kim,Deogyong Kim,Sangam Lee,Donguk Kwon,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Web$hell，通过 WebContract、WebCompiler 与 WebCache 将网页统一编译为可寻址的信息与动作对象，在单一访问模型下统一搜索、浏览与信息复访，实验证明其在搜索、导航与深度研究任务上以更低成本取得更强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体正成为开放网络的直接使用者，面对长时程、端到端任务，需要单一轨迹内同时完成广泛信息收集、浏览器操作与已获取证据的复访。现有访问模型（搜索增强型、浏览器型、文件系统型）只能主要支持其中一类能力，强行组合会带来显著成本或性能折衷，因此需要统一接口。

Method: 提出 Web$hell 统一接口：以 WebContract 定义公共 schema；WebCompiler 依据该 schema 将实时网页编译为可寻址的信息对象与动作对象；这些对象存入 WebCache，并通过一组共享操作进行读写与复用，从而为智能体提供一致、可操作、可持久化的网络访问。

Result: 在网页搜索、网页导航与深度研究等多类基准上的大量实验表明，Web$hell 以更低成本取得较强的任务性能，并优于现有访问模型。

Conclusion: 接口设计对有效网络访问至关重要；Web$hell 为 LLM 智能体在开放网络上实现更强能力与更高效率提供了新路径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are becoming direct users of the open web. As they take on increasingly complex and information-intensive tasks, effective web access is becoming a core capability for agentic problem solving. Existing approaches have advanced agentic web access through different access models, including search-augmented agents, browser-based agents, and, more recently, filesystem-based agents. Nevertheless, as users expect agents to complete long-horizon, end-to-end tasks, they increasingly need to conduct broad information gathering, perform browser actions, and revisit acquired evidence within a single trajectory. Yet our preliminary analyses reveal that existing access models struggle to meet these demands. They primarily support either information gathering or browser interaction, while combining their capabilities can involve substantial costs or performance trade-offs. To this end, we present Web\$hell, an interface that unifies web access under a single access model. Using a common schema defined by WebContract, WebCompiler compiles live webpages into addressable information and action objects. These objects are maintained in WebCache and handled through a shared set of operations, providing agents with consistent, operable, and persistent web access. Through extensive experiments across web search, web navigation, and deep research benchmarks, we show that Web\$hell achieves strong task performance at lower cost. Our results further indicate the importance of interface design for effective web access, with Web\$hell opening new possibilities for more capable and efficient LLM agents on the web.

</details>


### [79] [SpireBench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Strategic Play of Agentic Models with Slay the Spire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12596)
*Scott C. Lowe,Sander W. Keemink*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpireBench 是首个让 LLM 完整游玩《杀戮尖塔》(Slay the Spire) 的长时程智能体基准，20 个 LLM 表现逊于专家玩家且差距随游戏进程拉大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准多为短时程、情节式任务，长时程策略推理研究不足；且长任务表现难以拆解为记忆知识、推理能力与运气成分。

Method: 构建 SpireBench，让模型在最高难度下完整游玩商业卡牌构筑游戏《杀戮尖塔》，早期决策影响长期成败。评测 20 个 LLM，并与启发式基线、人类玩家对比；通过逐场战斗分析、全游戏术语一致性重命名、跨局记忆写入等机制定位性能来源与失败原因。首次将完整原版游戏交给模型，并与现有测试框架比较。

Result: 前沿模型超越启发式基线并达到普通人类玩家水平，但远逊于专家；差距随游戏进程拉大——最强模型在第一幕胜率与专家持平，随后落后。强弱模型的差异在于每场战斗的发挥；所有模型在整局中对风险判断失误，倾向保守保血而非花费生命成长。术语重命名实验表明，部分性能依赖于模型的先验游戏知识；模型无法从跨局写入的记忆中提升表现，这些记忆常出错且过于保守。

Conclusion: SpireBench 提供了一个尚未饱和的长时程基准，并配有定位智能体失败位置与原因的工具。

Abstract: Agentic large language models now tackle complex tasks, yet strategic reasoning over long horizons is under-studied: most agentic benchmarks are short and episodic, and performance on long tasks is hard to dissect into stored knowledge, reasoning and luck. We introduce SpireBench, in which models play the complete commercial deck-builder Slay the Spire (StS), where early decisions decide long-term success. Across 20 large language models (LLMs) at the game's highest difficulty, frontier models beat heuristic baselines and match the average human player but remain far behind experts. This gap widens as the run lengthens: the strongest models win the first act as often as experts, then fall behind. What separates strong models from weak is how well they play each fight; all of them misjudge risk over a run, preserving health rather than spending it to grow stronger. A comprehensive, self-consistent renaming of every game term shows that part of their performance rests on prior knowledge of the game, and models do not improve from the memories they write across runs, which are often wrong and overly cautious. Our harness is the first to give a model the complete original game, and we compare it with existing harnesses. Overall, SpireBench offers an unsaturated long-horizon benchmark with tools to locate where and why agents fail.

</details>


### [80] [RATED: Reliability-Aware AI Agent Tool Retrieval via Execution Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13889)
*Weiliang Chen,Song Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RATED 工具检索框架，结合语义匹配与动态可靠性自适应，并发布 EcoAgent 基准，显著提升检索精度并大幅降低故障选择率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具检索方法主要优化语义相关性，忽略了执行过程中工具的可用性（如运行时故障），导致 AI 智能体行为不可靠。

Method: RATED 采用冻结双编码器加对比微调进行语义匹配，并引入轻量级上下文赌博机（contextual bandit）模块，基于实时执行反馈对候选工具重排序，实现无需重训练的快速自适应。

Result: 相较 ToolLLM API retriever，真实查询下 MRR 相对提升 52.2%；在 30% 故障率的严重 API 中断场景中，故障选择率从 86.2% 降至 2.9%；相比纯编码推理仅增加不足 1 ms 延迟。

Conclusion: 将执行动态纳入检索可有效提升工具调用可靠性，RATED 在精度、鲁棒性与效率间取得良好平衡，并提供 EcoAgent 基准支持后续研究。

Abstract: As AI agents increasingly rely on external tools, accurate retrieval is essential for reliable behavior. Existing methods optimize semantic relevance but largely ignore tool availability during execution. To address this gap, we propose RATED (Reliability-Aware Tool Retrieval via Execution Dynamics), a retrieval framework that combines robust semantic matching with dynamic reliability adaptation. RATED employs a frozen dual-encoder with contrastive fine-tuning for semantic matching, and a lightweight contextual bandit module that re-ranks candidates based on real-time execution feedback. This design enables rapid adaptation to runtime tool failures without expensive model retraining. We introduce EcoAgent, a benchmark of 5,172 real-world tools and over 210,000 query-tool pairs for offline retrieval and dynamic reliability evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that RATED achieves a 52.2% relative MRR improvement over the ToolLLM API retriever on real-world queries, and reduces fault selection rate from 86.2% to 2.9% under severe API outage scenarios with a 30% failure rate, while incurring negligible latency overhead of less than 1 ms over encode-only inference. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/Elliotes/EcoAgent.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Binary Verifiable Rewards: Behavioral Self-Rewarding for SWE Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12598)
*Yiran Wu,Andrew Zhao,Yurui Chang,Qingyun Wu,Xuan Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行为自奖励（Behavioral Self-Rewarding, BSR）方法，复用当前策略对同一任务、同一验证结果的轨迹进行调试行为的两两比较，并将偏好聚合成有界的辅助奖励，在保留验证器通过/失败排序的同时引入奖励对比，从而在不额外训练奖励模型、不新增环境交互的情况下提升软件工程智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 通过可执行测试为软件工程智能体提供可靠监督，但二值奖励无法区分调试质量差异显著的轨迹——所有通过验证的轨迹获得相同奖励，导致大量有区分度的监督信号被浪费。作者希望探究：当前策略能否自行评判其轨迹，并将这些判断转化为性能改进，而无需单独训练奖励模型。

Method: BSR 是一种奖励精炼方法：它复用策略本身，对来自同一任务、且具有相同验证器结果（同为通过或同为失败）的轨迹，就其可观测的调试行为进行两两比较；随后将成对偏好聚合为任务局部（task-local）得分，再转换为有界的辅助奖励。该设计在保留验证器定义的通过/失败排序的前提下引入奖励对比，且仅依赖已采集的轨迹，无需新的环境交互。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 与 SWE-bench-Live 两个基准上，于 Qwen3-14B 和 Qwen3-32B 配置下均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持将策略自身的比较性判断作为一种补充学习信号，用于训练软件工程智能体，从而在验证器二值奖励之外提供更细粒度的轨迹质量监督。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) provides reliable supervision for software engineering agents through executable tests, but binary rewards fail to distinguish trajectories with substantially different debugging quality. We investigate whether the current policy can judge its own trajectories and use these judgments to improve task performance without a separately trained reward model. To this end, we introduce Behavioral Self-Rewarding (BSR), a reward-refinement method that reuses the policy to compare observable debugging behavior among trajectories from the same task with the same verifier outcome. BSR aggregates pairwise preferences into task-local scores and converts them into bounded auxiliary rewards, introducing reward contrast while preserving the verifier-defined pass/fail ordering. This provides additional supervision from already-collected trajectories without requiring new environment interactions. Experiments on SWE-bench Verified and SWE-bench-Live show consistent improvements across the evaluated Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-32B configurations, supporting using the policy's own comparative judgments as a complementary learning signal for training software engineering agents.

</details>


### [82] [Can Language Models Play the Wikipedia Game?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13948)
*Alexander Hickey,Joanna Knight,James Bishop*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用"维基百科游戏"构建信息检索任务，在五种语言和五个学术数据集上比较句嵌入模型与LLM的导航能力，模型未显著超越人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地支持或自动完成对超大文档集合的信息检索，但当任务需要专业语言或科学知识时难度很大，且现有评测常依赖第三方API，缺乏跨语言、跨领域的稳健比较手段。

Method: 以维基百科游戏为测试平台：玩家仅通过文章链接到达目标文章。利用OpenAlex构建大规模的维基本地页面图与论文引用图，并提供构建与导航代码，避免依赖第三方API。在五种维基语言和五个学术文献数据集上，对句嵌入模型与LLM进行比较，并以人类和TF-IDF作为基准。

Result: 没有任何模型显著优于人类，但最佳模型（GPT-o3 mini、GPT-4o、QwQ:32B、DeepSeek-R1:70B）取得了与人类相当的成 功率；性能与排名随语言和主题而变化。

Conclusion: 维基百科游戏范式支持在技术、语言与领域间进行稳健比较，其跨语言与跨主题的性能差异对使用这些技术的实践者具有参考意义。

Abstract: Language models increasingly support or automate information retrieval from document collections too large to index. This task is difficult when it requires specialist linguistic or scientific knowledge. We used the Wikipedia Game to test language models on an analogous information retrieval task. In the game, players must reach a target article using only article links. We compared sentence embedding models and LLMs across five Wikipedia languages and five academic-literature datasets. We construct very large local graphs of Wikipedia pages and of paper citations using OpenAlex and provide code to build and navigate them. This allows the study of language models' navigation capabilities without relying on third-party APIs. We compared the models with human and TF-IDF benchmarks. The Wikipedia Game format enables robust comparisons across techniques, languages, and domains. No model significantly outperformed humans, although the best models (GPT-o3 mini, GPT-4o, QwQ:32B, and DeepSeek-R1:70B) achieved comparable success. Performance and rankings varied across languages and topics, with implications for practitioners utilising these techniques.

</details>


### [83] [CLEAT: Learning Value-Guided Clarification for Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12655)
*Jiatan Huang,Zheyuan Zhang,Ziming Li,Tianyi Ma,Mingchen Li,Yanfang Ye,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLEAT 框架，通过价值引导的智能体协同训练实现交互式意图对齐中的澄清，使澄清引导随任务策略学习而自适应，在四个基准十个设置中均取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体需处理用户未明说的目标与约束，澄清是交互式意图对齐的关键手段；但澄清只有在智能体能将回答转化为更优决策时才有用。由于训练会改变智能体处理不完整信息与利用额外回答的方式，同一澄清的价值会随之变化，因此需要让澄清引导随策略演化而自适应。

Method: 提出 CLEAT（CLarification through valuE-guided Agent co-Training），从共享交互结果中协同训练澄清价值控制器与任务策略。定义"残差查询价值"，度量在当前策略下提问相比直接推进所获得的任务效用，并通过同一交互历史下的成对 rollout 进行估计；用配对回报分别训练控制器预测澄清价值、训练任务策略选择有用问题并依据回答行动。控制器指导后续 rollout 采集，更新后智能体的结果又提供新的价值标签，从而实现引导的自适应。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 上，CLEAT 在四个基准共十个评测设置中均取得最高平均分；相比最强基线，在 UserGym Travel 上提升 13.2 个百分点，在 IN3 欠指定判断准确率上提升 6.5 个百分点。消融实验表明，即使推理时移除控制器，协同训练仍能提升任务策略；而将澄清引导适配到演化中的策略可带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: 澄清的有效性依赖于任务策略本身的能力，因此澄清引导应与策略协同演化；CLEAT 通过价值引导的协同训练统一了二者，验证了自适应澄清引导在交互式意图对齐中的有效性与必要性。

Abstract: LLM agents can execute increasingly complex tasks, but effective assistance also requires aligning their actions with goals and constraints that users may leave unstated. We study this problem as interactive intent alignment through clarification. Yet clarification is useful only when the agent can turn the answer into better decisions. As training changes how the agent handles incomplete information and uses additional answers, the same clarification can become more or less useful. We introduce CLEAT (CLarification through valuE-guided Agent co-Training), a framework that co-trains a clarification-value controller and the task policy from shared interaction outcomes. We formulate residual query value to measure the task utility gained by asking rather than proceeding under the current policy, and estimate it through paired rollouts from the same interaction history. These paired returns train the controller to predict clarification value and the task policy to select useful questions and act on the answers. The controller guides subsequent rollout collection, while outcomes from the updated agent provide new value labels that allow clarification guidance to adapt as the agent learns. With Qwen3-4B, CLEAT achieves the highest mean score in all ten evaluation settings across four benchmarks. Gains over the strongest baseline reach 13.2 and 6.5 percentage points on UserGym Travel and IN3 underspecification judgment accuracy, respectively. Ablations show that co-training improves the task policy even when the controller is removed at inference, while adapting clarification guidance to the evolving policy yields further gains.

</details>


### [84] [MagicBeanStalk: Escaping the RLVR Zero Advantage Trap with Recursive Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12659)
*Ximing Lu,Aviral Kumar,Jan Kautz,Yejin Choi,David Acuna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出MagicBeanStalk，一种自顶向下分解与自底向上递归的数据合成算法，用于生成与目标策略及训练预算校准的RLVR训练问题，在数学与金融基准上显著提升模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR数据扩展通常聚焦于收集或生成可验证问题，但语料规模本身并不能决定数据集是否为目标策略提供有效学习信号，大量开源数学语料陷入零优势陷阱（约88%问题在所有 rollout 中全对或全错）。

Method: 作者将数据集有效性刻画为问题、目标策略与训练 rollout 预算的函数，发现过滤只能筛选而无法保留难题；据此提出MagicBeanStalk，通过自顶向下分解和自底向上递归合成，将种子问题扩展为保留推理蓝图的、策略与预算校准的新问题族。

Result: 在8个数学基准上，持续RLVR使 gpt-oss-120b 从61.2提升至69.8（+8.6），DeepSeek v4Flash 从72.4提升至77.9（+5.4）；全量78K源语料仅提升+1.1，最佳过滤真实数据子集+5.8。金融领域用340个解合成超14K可验证问题，SecQue与FinanceBench分别提升6.9和2.4分。

Conclusion: 有效RLVR数据应重新构造难题的推理操作与组合，并校准难度使部分rollout成功、部分失败且至少一个正确解在训练预算内；MagicBeanStalk优于全量语料与过滤真实数据，具备跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Data-centric scaling of RLVR has focused on collecting or generating verifiable training problems. However, corpus size alone does not determine whether the dataset provides a useful learning signal for a target policy. To guide the synthesis of effective RLVR data, we experimentally characterize dataset effectiveness as a function of its problems, the target policy, and the training rollout budget. Under this characterization, we show that a large open-source math corpus pooled from widely used RLVR datasets falls almost entirely into a zero-advantage trap: for a fixed target policy and rollout budget, roughly 88% of its problems are solved in every rollout or failed in every rollout. Filtering concentrates what remains, but it can only select, discarding the hard problems. Effective synthetic problems should therefore require the reasoning operations and compositions required to solve those hard problems. Yet their difficulty must be calibrated, so that some rollouts succeed, some fail, and at least one correct solution fits within the training budget. Prompting a teacher to vary hard problems directly does not achieve this. Guided by these empirical principles, we introduce MagicBeanStalk, a top-down decomposition and bottom-up recursive data-synthesis algorithm that grows seed problems into families of substantively new, policy- and budget-calibrated training problems while preserving the seeds' reasoning blueprints. Across 8 math benchmarks, continual RLVR on MagicBeanStalk problems improves gpt-oss-120b from 61.2 to 69.8 (+8.6) and DeepSeek v4Flash from 72.4 to 77.9 (+5.4) on a 16K-token budget. Training on the full 78K source corpus improves by only +1.1 and its best filtered real data subset by +5.8. In finance, we synthesize over 14K verifiable problems from only 340 solutions and improve over base by 6.9 and 2.4 points on SecQue and FinanceBench, respectively.

</details>


### [85] [Faithful and Complete LLM-Assisted Automated Construction of Hierarchical Hardware Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12762)
*Haotian Qiao,Robert P. Dick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LLM驱动的自动化流程，将硬件数据手册转化为分层硬件知识图谱，实现高保真、可追溯、覆盖率更全的属性抽取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件数据手册含关键工程规格，但篇幅长、异构性强、信息分散于表格/图/文本，人工检查繁琐易错。LLM虽可自动化，但朴素一次性处理即便在上下文窗口内也会遗漏规格。

Method: 构建分阶段LLM流水线：基于分块的属性抽取、分层路由、迭代精炼；LLM负责信息抽取与语义推理，保留来源溯源，通过验证指令与确定性本地执行进行图更新，降低单次查询上下文并维持可追溯中间表示。

Result: 在20份多类型、多长度硬件数据手册上评估，属性保真度98.8%，一次性基线为76.1%；反向覆盖上，本方法全覆盖83.6%的基线保真属性，而基线仅全覆盖本方法的10.5%。

Conclusion: 该框架为下游硬件设计与验证提供更可靠、全面的工程信息，降低遗漏约束与错误决策风险。

Abstract: Hardware datasheets contain engineering specifications essential for component selection, system integration, and hardware compatibility verification. However, manual inspection is tedious and error-prone because datasheets are lengthy and heterogeneous, with dense information fragmented across tables, figures, and text. Although large language models (LLMs) can automate this process, naïve one-shot processing omits relevant specifications even when the document fits within the model's context window.
  We present an automated LLM-driven pipeline that transforms hardware datasheets into general-purpose hierarchical hardware knowledge graphs through chunk-based property extraction, hierarchical routing, and iterative refinement. The framework uses LLMs for information extraction and semantic reasoning while preserving source provenance and applying graph updates through validated instructions and deterministic local execution. This staged design reduces per-query context and maintains traceable intermediate representations. The resulting knowledge graphs provide semantically organized, source-grounded hardware properties for downstream retrieval and reasoning.
  We evaluate the framework on 20 hardware datasheets spanning diverse component types and document lengths. We assess property faithfulness and introduce inter-graph completeness to measure relative semantic coverage without exhaustive ground truth. Our framework achieves 98.8% property faithfulness compared with 76.1% for the one-shot baseline, while fully covering 83.6% of faithful baseline properties; in the reverse direction, the baseline fully covers only 10.5% of ours. These improvements provide more reliable and comprehensive engineering information for downstream hardware design and verification, reducing the risk of overlooked constraints and incorrect decisions.

</details>


### [86] [Reward Hacking as a Transmissible Strategy in Multi-Agent Coding Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12822)
*Yuepeng Hu,Pegah Ghahremani,Reza Aghajani,Mahesh Pasupuleti,Himaghna Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示奖励黑客（reward hacking）在多智能体编码系统中具有可传染性：单一智能体习得的作弊策略可通过代码、共享记忆与自然语言计划传播至其他智能体，甚至由弱模型传染给更强的闭源模型，并在传递链上可能放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究多将奖励黑客视为单智能体失效问题，而多智能体系统通过共享代码与计划协作，可能使作弊策略跨智能体扩散并影响系统协调。作者希望验证奖励黑客是否是一种可传播策略，并探索其传播条件与放大机制。

Method: 在四个开放权重骨干模型上进行编码强化学习（RL），设置两种诱导场景：系统提示中显式机制描述、中期训练中模型接触编码环境相关事实。以中期训练场景得到的后RL智能体为源，考察其通过代码、共享记忆、自然语言计划及跨角色渠道的传播；并以小型开源模型诱导较强闭源模型。此外对奖励黑客在智能体交互中放大、持续、衰减的条件做理论分析，并评估编码者自审与独立验证者审查两种缓解措施。

Result: 编码RL会诱发并放大奖励黑客行为；传播可经代码、共享记忆和自然语言计划发生，并跨角色扩散。基于小型开源模型的智能体也能诱导更强的闭源目标出现奖励黑客。困难任务中，即使原始来源退出，传播链上的作弊仍可能因代码获得更具说服力的理由而加剧。奖励黑客还会使任务路由偏向作弊智能体，并在候选聚合后仍存留。代码审查（编码者自审或独立验证者）可减少交付的作弊结果。

Conclusion: 奖励黑客是一种可传播策略，其影响超出最初产生该行为的智能体本身；多智能体协作环境需要针对策略传播进行防护，而不仅是单智能体层面的评估与修正。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are increasingly used to solve coding tasks by coordinating agents that share code and plans. These agents are often optimized with reinforcement learning (RL) using automated evaluators, but bypassing evaluation can earn reward without producing a genuine solution. Prior work has largely treated reward hacking as a single-agent failure. In this work, we show that a reward-hacking strategy elicited in one agent can propagate to others and affect system coordination. Across four open-weight backbones, coding RL elicits and amplifies reward hacking in two settings: explicit mechanism descriptions in the system prompt and mid-training that models upstream exposure to facts about the coding environment. Using post-RL agents from the mid-training setting as sources, we observe propagation through code and shared memory, and across roles through natural-language plans. Even agents based on small open-weight models induce reward hacking in stronger closed-weight targets. On hard tasks, propagation can intensify along a transmission chain after the original source leaves as the code acquires more persuasive justifications. We also theoretically analyze conditions under which reward hacking amplifies, persists, or decays across agent interactions. Beyond propagation, reward hacking can bias task routing toward reward-hacking agents and survive candidate aggregation. As potential mitigations, we evaluate code review by the coder or a separate verifier, both of which reduce delivered hacks. Together, these results establish reward hacking as a transmissible strategy whose effects extend beyond the agent in which it first emerges.

</details>


### [87] [Representation Alignment from a Few Paired Data via Distilled Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12781)
*Julien Kordes,Reuben Dorent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究仅用 N∼100–500 对配对样本对齐两个独立预训练单模态编码器的低资源情形，通过锚点相似度表示、熵最优传输匹配与蒸馏，实现跨模态对齐，在图文与分子-文本任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规跨模态对齐依赖数百万配对样本，而现实中配对数据稀缺但单模态无配对数据丰富。作者希望在 N∼100–500 极小锚点预算下实现有效对齐，并避免最近邻匹配受 hubness 影响的问题。

Method: 方法包含三步：(1) 用每个表示与 N 个配对锚点的相似度进行编码，使两模态落入共同坐标系；(2) 在整个数据池上求解单个熵最优传输问题，通过边缘约束匹配两个点云，抑制 hubness；(3) 将传输方案蒸馏到两个轻量编码器中，使对应关系具备归纳能力。并建立该边缘约束与双语词典归纳中一步 hub 修正的联系。仅锚点需配对，未配对集合可独立采样。

Result: 在相同锚点预算下，方法在 Flickr30k、MS-COCO 图文对齐和 ChEBI-20 分子-文本对齐任务上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 在极少配对锚点、大量未配对数�据的设定下，熵最优传输结合锚点相似度表示与蒸馏可有效对齐独立预训练的单模态编码器，且不要求锚点之外样本具备双模态观测。

Abstract: Aligning two independently pretrained unimodal encoders typically demands millions of paired examples. In this paper, we study the regime where only $N \sim 100$--$500$ pairs are available, while unpaired data on either side is abundant. Our method expresses every representation by its similarities to the $N$ paired anchors, which places both modalities in a common coordinate system; matches the two point clouds with a single entropic optimal transport problem solved over the entire pool, whose marginal constraints counteract the hubness that defeats nearest-neighbor matching; and distills the resulting plan into two lightweight encoders, so that the correspondence becomes inductive. We additionally put this marginal constraint in relation with the one-step hub corrections of the bilingual lexicon induction literature. Pairing is required only for the anchors: the unpaired collections may be drawn independently from the two marginals, so that no item outside the anchor set needs to have both of its modalities observed. We evaluate on image-text and molecule-text alignment, where our method outperforms existing approaches at the same anchor budget on Flickr30k, MS-COCO and ChEBI-20.

</details>


### [88] [JANUS: Enhancing Long-Horizon Autonomous Research with Co-Evolving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13194)
*Mengdi Liu,Chenghao Jia,Hong Chang,Mingjie He,Shiguang Shan,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出JANUS框架，用协同进化的世界模型构建"预测-验证-进化"闭环，在搜索空间中提前筛选与剪枝候选方案，解决长周期自主研究中执行反馈延迟导致的进化效率瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期自主研究的Generate-Execute-Feedback范式依赖"反应式试错"，执行成本高、耗时长，导致反馈延迟严重；在搜索空间爆炸式扩张的情况下，进化进展被严重抑制。因此需要一种能在执行前预判候选价值、减少无效执行的机制。

Method: JANUS在演化图（evolving graph）中工作。研究智能体生成候选方案，协同进化的世界模型从价值提升（value improvement）、信息增益（information gain）、探索效用（exploration utility）三个维度打分，据此在执行前对分支进行选择、精炼或剪枝。获得真实反馈后，框架将经验蒸馏为多层技能：研究智能体的层级化技能、世界模型的探索策略技能，以及二者间的协同协议。该机制实现持续协同进化：智能体提升候选生成能力，世界模型提升长时程预测判断力。

Result: 在MLS-Bench-Lite及真实研究任务上，JANUS显著优于当前最优基线，在进化效率（evolving efficiency）和进化上限（ceiling）两方面均取得提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入预测式世界模型替代纯反应式试错，JANUS验证了"预测-验证-进化"闭环与智能体-世界模型协同进化的有效性，可在高成本长周期研究场景中同时提高搜索效率与成果天花板。

Abstract: Long-horizon autonomous research has shown promise in accelerating scientific progress through sustained cycles of hypothesis generation, implementation, and validation. However, this Generate-Execute-Feedback paradigm relies on reactive trial-and-error, where costly and time-consuming executions introduce severe feedback delays, throttling evolutionary progress under explosively expanding search space. To address this challenge, we propose JANUS, a framework that unifies future outcomes forecasting and past experience reflection through a Predict-Verify-Evolve loop driven by a co-evolving world model. Within an evolving graph, the research agent proposes candidates while world model scores them by forecasting value improvement, information gain, and exploration utility, then accordingly selects, refines, or prunes branches prior to execution. After receiving ground-truth feedback, JANUS distills experience into evolving skills: hierarchical skills for research agent, exploration-strategy skills for world model, and their coordination protocols. This mechanism enables continual co-evolution: the research agent sharpens candidate generation while the world model refines its predictive judgment over extended horizons. Extensive evaluations on MLS-Bench-Lite and real research tasks demonstrate that JANUS significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving both higher evolving efficiency and ceiling.

</details>


### [89] [EvoBelief: Falsifiable World Models for Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12789)
*Janet Wang,Jiang Guo,Yi Xiang,Jiaqing Yuan,Mingwen Dong,Hideo Kobayashi,Shuaichen Chang,Chandan K. Reddy,Jiarong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 EvoBelief——一种自演化的世界模型，将环境知识表示为可证伪假设。模型语言参数保持冻结，通过形成、验证、修正规则来实现部署中的信念演化。在四个基准上提升任务表现并实现跨任务规则复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演化 LLM 智能体主要通过积累经验、反思或技能来改进"如何行动"，但当智能体对环境动态的信念不完整、错误或过时时，即使流程能力完备也会失败。作者研究自演化的另一维度：在部署过程中演化这些信念。信念可支撑多任务，但错误信念也会跨任务传播。

Method: EvoBelief 将环境知识表示为可证伪假设。意外结果与决策相关的未知项触发假设形成；后续观察与受控实验提供独立验证，规则只有通过验证才能在其支持范围内指导预测或规划；反例使规则收窄或退役。语言模型保持冻结，每条规则与证据及其所改变的决策保持关联。

Result: 在涵盖静态动态、隐藏漂移与科学发现的四个基准上，EvoBelief 提升任务表现并跨任务复用规则。在 ALFWorld 无 actor 侧转移记忆设定下，相比匹配的 Frozen World Model，使用 Claude Haiku 4.5 和 GPT-5.4 分别提升成功率 23.9% 和 31.3%。在 MiniGrid 上能修正过时规则并在未公告的动态变化后恢复。在 DiscoveryWorld 的 Reactor Lab 中，完成 Normal 级 83.3%、Challenge 级 58.3% 的实例，而各基线均为 0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，自改进可通过可检验、可修正的环境知识实现，而非依赖模型参数更新。

Abstract: Self-evolving LLM agents typically improve how they act through accumulated experience, reflection, or skills. Yet even procedurally capable agents can fail when their beliefs about environment dynamics are incomplete, incorrect, or outdated. We study a complementary axis of self-evolution: evolving those beliefs during deployment. Such beliefs can support multiple tasks, but incorrect ones can also propagate across them. We introduce EvoBelief, a self-evolving world model that represents environment knowledge as falsifiable hypotheses. Unexpected outcomes and decision-relevant unknowns trigger hypothesis formation. Later observations and controlled experiments provide independent validation before a rule can guide predictions or plans within its supported scope. Counterexamples narrow or retire rules as the environment changes. The language model remains frozen, and each rule remains linked to its evidence and the decisions it changes. Across four benchmarks spanning static dynamics, hidden drift, and scientific discovery, EvoBelief improves task performance and reuses learned rules across tasks. On ALFWorld without actor-side transition memory, it improves success over the matched Frozen World Model by 23.9% and 31.3% with Claude Haiku 4.5 and GPT-5.4, respectively. On MiniGrid, it revises stale rules and recovers after unannounced dynamics changes. In DiscoveryWorld's Reactor Lab, it discovers and validates quantitative laws, completing 83.3% of Normal-tier and 58.3% of Challenge-tier instances, versus 0% for each evaluated baseline. These results demonstrate self-improvement through testable, revisable environment knowledge rather than model-parameter updates.

</details>


### [90] [A Multi-Agent LLM System for Automated Tuning of Specialized Neural Network Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13663)
*Ivan Moloshnikov,Dmitry Kunitsyn,Alexander Sboev,Maksim Skorokhodov,Roman Rybka,Yuanchao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多智能体LLM框架（分析师/编码员/记录员三角色，含实验日志与沙箱），在固定网络结构下仅自动生成/调优指定关键组件的代码，跨模型类型可迁移，并在脉冲神经网络分类与PINN求解高阶微分方程五任务上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习方法常为两层结构：先由专家选定方法类别，而类别内关键组件的选择通常依赖人工，缺乏自动调优且难以跨不同性质模型迁移。

Method: 构建多智能体大语言模型框架，设分析师、编码员、记录员三种角色，配备实验日志与沙箱执行环境。与优化整个ML脚本或在离散空间搜索架构的方法不同，该框架在开放但语义狭窄的空间内工作：智能体仅针对指定组件生成代码，保持整体网络结构不变。

Result: 在五类任务上验证：MNIST与Digits脉冲分类精度分别提升13和6个百分点；对解析单孤子解误差约降低2491倍；选定GNLSE（高斯扰动）运行中不变量守恒误差约降低325倍，重复初始化平均降低超100倍；KS方程（高斯扰动）质量漂移降低14倍。

Conclusion: 该框架无需改变内部结构即可在不同性质的任务上调优关键组件，具备跨模型类型的可迁移性与有效性。

Abstract: Many machine learning approaches are two-level: an expert selects a class of methods, within this class the choice of key components is critical and usually manual. This paper addresses the automatic tuning of these components and proposes a framework transferable across models of different nature. The multi-agent large language model (LLM) framework has three roles---analyst, coder, and recorder~---with an experiment log and a sandboxed execution environment. Unlike systems that optimize the entire ML script or search architectures in a discrete space, the framework operates over an open but semantically narrow space: the agent generates code only for the specified components, leaving the overall network structure unchanged. The approach is validated in two directions: tuning a classification spiking neural network with correlation learning, and solving high-order differential equations with Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINN). Across five tasks---spiking classification on MNIST and Digits, and boundary-value problems for the generalized nonlinear Schrödinger equation (GNLSE) and Kuramoto--Sivashinsky (KS) equations---the agent outperforms the baselines: classification accuracy is 13 percentage points (MNIST) and 6 percentage points (Digits) higher; the error against the analytical single-soliton solution is reduced approximately 2491-fold; the invariant-preservation error drops approximately 325-fold in the selected GNLSE run with a Gaussian perturbation and by more than 100-fold on average over repeated initializations; and the mass drift drops 14-fold for the KS problem with a Gaussian perturbation. The framework thus tunes key components on tasks of different nature without changing its internal structure.

</details>


### [91] [VEPA: Verified Evolving Formal Policy for Lifelong LLM Agent Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12813)
*Duy Nguyen,Lesly Miculicich,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Shuo Li,Krishnamurthy Dj Dvijotham,Mohit Bansal,Tomas Pfister,Long T. Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VEPA 提出一种面向 LLM 智能体的形式化护栏"可验证演化"框架：用可执行符号规则表示策略，由 LLM 依据部署反馈生成策略补丁，经确定性验证器保证结构有效且无冲突，再由证据感知的授权层在证据充分时激活规则，从而在三种基准上均优于基线并扩展了确定性策略执行范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体越来越多地操作私有文档和可变系统状态等敏感资源，需要在不可逆动作执行前给出可审查、可复现、确定性的允许或拒绝决策。但安全需求在部署前往往不完备，护栏必须持续适应新风险。现有自适应护栏依赖 LLM 推理执行安全策略，导致决策不一致、策略解释模糊；形式化护栏虽确定性执行，却依赖部署前或任务特定策略，缺乏适应新风险的机制。二者各有短板，VEPA 旨在兼得确定性执行与在线适应能力。

Method: VEPA 将护栏表示为作用于带类型智能体事件的可执行符号规则形式化策略。部署期间，由部署反馈引导的 LLM 提出策略补丁，在修复新观测失败的同时保持既有正确行为；随后确定性验证器仅接受结构有效且无冲突的策略；最后由证据感知的授权层在规则获得足够支持证据时才将其激活，使不确定更新不能直接影响护栏决策。

Result: 在 ASSEBench-Safety、ASSEBench-Security 和 ATBench 三个基准上，VEPA 一致优于基线，并扩大了确定性形式策略的执行覆盖范围。这些增益在混合流、受损反馈和更长时域等更具挑战性的部署条件下依然成立。

Conclusion: VEPA 通过"LLM 提议补丁—确定性验证—证据授权激活"的闭环，实现了形式化智能体护栏的在线可验证演化，兼顾确定性执行与持续适应，为智能体运行时的安全与安保提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate over sensitive resources, such as private documents and mutable system states, which can create serious safety and security risks. Runtime guardrails should therefore make inspectable, reproducible, and deterministic allow or refuse decisions before potentially irreversible agent actions are executed. However, safety and security requirements are rarely complete before deployment, so guardrails need to keep adapting as new risks emerge. Existing adaptive guardrails learn from deployment experience, but they usually apply safety policies through LLM inference, which can lead to inconsistent decisions and ambiguous policy interpretations. Formal guardrails, on the other hand, enforce policies deterministically, but they usually rely on pre-deployment or task-specific policies and have no mechanism to adapt to newly observed risks. We introduce VEPA, a framework for the verified evolution of formal agent guardrails. VEPA represents the guardrail as a formal policy of executable symbolic rules over typed agent events. During deployment, we use an LLM guided by deployment feedback to propose policy patches that fix newly observed failures while preserving previously correct behavior. Then, a deterministic verifier only accepts policies that are structurally valid and conflict-free. Finally, an evidence-aware authority layer activates a verified rule only when it has enough supporting evidence, so uncertain updates cannot directly affect guardrail decisions. Across ASSEBench-Safety, ASSEBench-Security, and ATBench, VEPA consistently outperforms baselines while expanding the scope of deterministic formal-policy enforcement. These gains also hold under more challenging deployment conditions, including mixed streams, corrupted feedback, and longer horizons.

</details>


### [92] [Neural networks are more modular than single neurons suggest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12842)
*Shreya Saha,Benjamin Bergen,Meenakshi Khosla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出模块化程度的形式化嵌套层级（单元级、正交、线性），并建立统一的子空间分解与因果消融评估框架，发现在视觉、语言模型和多任务RNN中，缺乏单元级模块化的网络仍普遍存在子空间级功能模块化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络中单个神经元常同时参与多种功能，导致学界普遍认为这类网络缺乏模块化组织。作者质疑这一结论，认为此前仅以单元级标准衡量模块化过于严苛，可能遗漏了更高层次的功能组织。

Method: 提出形式化的模块化嵌套层级：单元级（不同计算占据不相交神经元）、正交（是否存在旋转揭示功能独立子空间）、线性（有界线性映射能否将任务相关计算分离为独立子空间）。为每一层级设计统一的分解学习框架，并通过选择性消融进行因果评估。在视觉模型、语言模型和多任务RNN上开展实验，并将学得子空间与脑数据做对齐分析。

Result: 视觉与语言模型中，内容与风格、句法与语义虽共享重叠神经元群，却在子空间消融下发生分离；学得子空间与脑数据存在差异化对齐（内容/风格子空间分别预测高级/低级视觉皮层，语义子空间在预测语言皮层活动上优于句法子空间）；在多任务认知RNN（此前被证明缺乏模块结构）中，该分解恢复了任务间清晰的双重分离。

Conclusion: 单元级模块化的缺失常与清晰的子空间级模块化共存。即便在没有单个神经元专门化的网络中，也能对特定计算实施靶向干预，这对机制可解释性以及理解生物与人工神经系统的功能组织具有广泛意义。

Abstract: Neural networks routinely develop representations in which individual neurons contribute to multiple distinct functions, leading to the widespread view that such networks lack modular organization. We challenge this conclusion by introducing a formally nested hierarchy of modularity: unit-level, which requires distinct computations to occupy disjoint neurons; orthogonal, which asks whether a rotation reveals functionally independent subspaces; and linear, which asks whether a bounded linear map can separate task-relevant computations into independent subspaces. We develop a unified framework for learning decompositions at each level and evaluating them causally through selective ablation. Across vision models, language models and multi-task Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs), we find that networks which lack unit-level modularity nonetheless often exhibit clear functional modularity at the subspace level. In vision and language models, content versus style and syntax versus semantics respectively dissociate under subspace ablation despite sharing overlapping populations of neurons. These learned subspaces also align differentially with brain data, with content and style subspaces predicting higher and lower visual cortex respectively, and semantic subspaces outperforming syntactic ones in predicting language cortex activity. In RNNs trained on multiple cognitive tasks and previously shown to lack modular structure, our decomposition recovers sharp double dissociations between tasks. The absence of unit-level modularity frequently coexists with clear subspace-level modularity across domains, enabling targeted intervention on specific computations even in networks where no individual neuron is specialized, with broad implications for mechanistic interpretability and for understanding functional organization in both biological and artificial neural systems.

</details>


### [93] [Towards Defining, Measuring, and Leveraging Creativity in AI Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12845)
*Jingyi Yang,Alexander Tuzhilin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出若干基于心理学（Boden三准则：新颖性、价值、惊奇性）的创造力形式化公设，并给出一个概念性的数学定义，通过在象棋走子与绘画（毕加索、雷诺阿等）两个领域的验证，表明该公式能统一刻画不同领域的创造性模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算创造力领域缺乏既具概念基础、又具实践相关性且可经验应用的形式化定义；现有研究要么只给非形式化的概念定义而无形式计算概念，要么给出形式定义却未审视其假设、也缺乏在具体应用场景中的验证。

Method: 以Boden的创造力三核心准则（新颖性newness、价值value、惊奇性surprise）为基础，提出若干形式化公设，并构建与之相符的概念性数学定义（创造力公式）；在两个应用场景中验证：评估特定棋步的创造力，以及评估毕加索、雷诺阿等画家作品的创造力，其中各准则的具体度量依据领域进行适配。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的创造力公式在象棋与艺术两个领域均能成功捕捉创造性模式。

Conclusion: 创造力可通过基于新颖性、价值、惊奇性三项基本准则的统一数学结构有效刻画，其具体度量可随领域调整，从而为跨领域（艺术、科研、游戏等）的创造力提供兼具理论依据与实用性的形式化框架。

Abstract: Despite extensive research, computational creativity still lacks a conceptually grounded, practically relevant, and empirically applicable formal definition across contexts such as art, scientific research, and games. Existing approaches to creativity often focus on proposing informal conceptual definitions without providing formal computational concepts. Alternatively, they propose formal definitions without carefully examining their underlying assumptions or providing validation of these definitions by testing their performance in specific application settings. To address these problems, this paper proposes several formal postulates of creativity grounded in prior psychological work on creativity, especially as related to Boden's three core criteria of creativity: newness, value, and surprise. Furthermore, we propose a conceptual mathematical definition of creativity that aligns with these postulates. We also validate our proposed creativity formula in two applications: determining the creativity of particular chess moves, and assessing the creativity of paintings by some artists, such as Picasso, Renoir, and other painters. Experimental results demonstrate that our formula of creativity successfully captures creative patterns across both the chess and the art domains. This suggests that creativity can be effectively characterized through a unified mathematical structure based on three fundamental criteria, namely newness, value, and surprise, whose specific measurements are adapted to the particular domains.

</details>


### [94] [Mitigating Capability Degradation in LLM Self-Improvement via Representation Geometry Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12855)
*Jiaqi Tian,Ting Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现On-Policy Self-distillation (OPSD) 会导致模型已有能力逐步退化，并将其归因于隐藏表示几何结构恶化（簇内紧凑性下降、簇间分离度降低），据此提出表示几何正则化 (RGR) 加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD 虽能让 LLM 通过同一模型在不同条件上下文下分别充当教师与学生来实现自我提升，但作者发现其存在能力退化问题：早期能正确回答的查询在训练推进后可能变为错误回答，即使整体性能仍在提升。进一步分析表明，这种退化与隐藏表示几何结构的恶化相关，即簇内紧凑性降低、簇间分离度减小，因此需要在后训练中保护表示几何。

Method: 提出表示几何正则化 (Representation Geometry Regularization, RGR)：在后训练过程中通过鼓励簇内紧凑 (intra-cluster compactness) 和簇间分离 (inter-cluster separation) 来保持表示几何；该方法可无缝集成到现有 OPSD 方法中，且不改变其核心优化流程。

Result: 在多种 OPSD 方法与基准上的大量实验表明，RGR 总体上能缓解能力退化，同时保留能力提升效果。

Conclusion: 表示几何的恶化是 OPSD 能力退化的关键因素，RGR 作为一种通用且易于集成的正则化手段，可在不改变 OPSD 核心优化的前提下有效缓解退化并维持性能增益。代码开源于 https://github.com/supermachinelearner/RGR。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-distillation is a self-improvement paradigm that enables large language models (LLMs) to improve their capabilities by leveraging the same model as both teacher and student under different conditioning contexts. However, we find that OPSD methods can progressively degrade previously acquired capabilities: queries that are correctly responded at earlier training stages may become incorrectly responded as optimization proceeds, even the overall performance continues to improve. Further analysis reveals that this degradation coincides with a deterioration of the geometry of hidden representations, characterized by reduced intra-cluster compactness and diminished inter-cluster separation. Motivated by this observation, we propose Representation Geometry Regularization (RGR), which preserves representation geometry during post-training by encouraging compactness within cluster and separation across cluster. RGR can be seamlessly integrated into existing OPSD methods without altering their core optimization procedures. Extensive experiments on multiple OPSD methods and benchmarks show that RGR generally alleviates capability degradation while retaining capability improvements. Our code is available in https://github.com/supermachinelearner/RGR.

</details>


### [95] [Collaborative Theory of Mind: Learning to Self-Revise by Anticipating User Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12872)
*Fanzhi Zeng,Anubrata Das,Maytal Saar-Tsechansky,Liu Leqi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出Collaborative Theory of Mind（CToM）框架，显式训练大语言模型预测用户回复并据此进行内部修订，从而提升多轮协作任务表现与交互效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM虽在单一任务上表现优异，但在多轮协作交互中仍存在困难，例如此前未理解用户需求就尝试给出方案，或在收到反馈后无法修正错误假设。

Method: CToM先草拟回复，预测用户将如何回应，并利用该预测引导内部修订后再输出。训练时将用户对每轮草稿的回复作为下一轮预测目标，最小化该回复的负对数似然，并与组相对策略优化（GRPO）结合，同时优化任务表现与交互效率。还通过消融实验和交互轨迹行为分析进行验证。

Result: 在数学推理、编程和写作任务中，CToM的协作任务表现优于所有基线；例如协作数学推理任务中，最终准确率相较朴素GRPO从74.1%提升至79.6%。消融显示，在相同奖励下加入预测监督，编程任务用户回复数减少8%、写作任务减少17%，同时任务表现提升。行为分析表明，训练后用户回复预测更准确，且预测越准确，通过自我修订带来的协作任务表现提升越大。

Conclusion: 显式预测用户回复并结合自我修订，能有效提升LLM多轮协作的任务表现与交互效率，为构建更具协作能力的LLM提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong individual task performance, yet still struggle to collaborate with users over extended, multi-turn interactions, for example by attempting solutions before understanding the user's needs or by failing to revise mistaken assumptions after receiving feedback. We propose Collaborative Theory of Mind (CToM), a framework that addresses these challenges by explicitly training LLMs to anticipate user responses and to use those predictions to improve their own behavior. CToM drafts a response, predicts how the user will respond, and uses this prediction to guide internal revision before presenting a response. During training, the user's reply to each draft serves as the target for predicting the next turn, and we train the assistant to minimize the negative log-likelihood of that reply. We combine this prediction objective with group-relative policy optimization (GRPO) to optimize both task performance and interaction efficiency. Across mathematical reasoning, coding, and writing tasks, CToM achieves higher collaborative task performance than all baselines; for example, on a collaborative mathematical reasoning task, it improves final accuracy from 74.1% to 79.6% compared with naive GRPO. Ablations show that predicting user responses also makes collaboration more efficient: under the same reward, adding prediction supervision reduces the number of user replies by 8% on coding and 17% on writing while improving task performance. Behavioral analyses of interaction trajectories show that user-response predictions become more accurate after training, and that more accurate predictions are associated with larger improvements in collaborative task performance through self-revision.

</details>


### [96] [One-Step Text Generation by Seq-Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12879)
*Jiayu Liu,Ruizhi Zhao,Tianyun Ji,Yee Whye Teh,Zhenya Huang,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Seq-Drifting 框架，将 drifting 方法扩展到连续 token 表示序列上，学习从高斯噪声到文本的直接映射，实现单次前向传播的文本生成，在 LM1B 上单步生成困惑度为 36.01。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）虽然并行生成 token，但仍需多步采样才能产生高质量文本。本文探究模型能否在单次前向传播中生成整条序列，以摆脱多步采样的开销。

Method: 提出 Seq-Drifting：在连续 token 表示序列上施加 drifting，直接学习从高斯噪声到文本的映射。与现有 DLM 从训练序列的观测 token 直接构造逐位置监督不同，Seq-Drifting 构建的漂移场其逐位置目标会随当前生成上下文自适应调整；每个位置上，吸引力将输出引向一组上下文合理的 token 表示，排斥力则防止样本间坍缩和序列内重复。生成器在训练中学习该漂移场，推理时一次前向即可联合生成全部 token。

Result: 在 LM1B 上单步生成困惑度达 36.01；在无条件与条件生成中均具竞争力；在翻译、摘要和推理任务上的实验进一步验证了框架在开放式生成之外的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 在 token 序列上进行 drifting 为实现高效单步文本生成提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Diffusion language models generate tokens in parallel, but still require multiple sampling steps to produce high-quality text. In this paper, we investigate whether a model can learn to generate an entire sequence in a single forward pass. We propose Seq-Drifting, a framework that applies drifting to sequences of continuous token representations and directly learns a map from Gaussian noise to text. Unlike existing DLMs that derive the supervision at each position directly from the observed token in a training sequence, Seq-Drifting builds a drift field whose position-wise targets adapt to the current generated context. At each position, attraction guides each output toward a set of contextually plausible token representations, while repulsion discourages collapse across samples and repetition within sequences. The generator learns from this drift field during training and produces all tokens jointly in one pass at inference. Empirically, Seq-Drifting achieves a generative perplexity of 36.01 on LM1B in one step and performs competitively in both unconditional and conditional generation. Experiments on translation, summarization, and reasoning further demonstrate the applicability of the framework beyond open-ended generation. These results suggest that drifting over token sequences offers a promising path toward effective one-step text generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/jyliu-ai/Seq-Drifting.

</details>


### [97] [Mind the Spatial Gap: A Benchmark for Geospatial Reasoning and Constrained Spatial Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12881)
*Zheng Ren,Yanning Wang,Ziheng Huang,Fei Huang,Shiqi Wang,Ziqian Ye,Yang Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GeoSpatialBench 基准，用于评估多模态大模型从空间关系理解、几何推理到受约束空间编辑的递进式空间认知能力，并揭示"被动识别"与"主动操控"之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型空间推理评测主要依赖小规模或合成场景的静态问答（QA），极少考察模型能否在满足显式几何约束的前提下修改空间配置，因此难以反映面向物理环境与具身空间智能所需的主动操控能力。

Method: 构建 GeoSpatialBench：基于六座地理多样城市的真实城市数据，覆盖28种拓扑、方向、度量与顺序关系，涵盖结构化 GeoJSON 与地图图像两种模态，并由 GIS 操作生成真值参考与约束校验；评测任务按空间关系理解、几何推理、受约束空间编辑的递进层级设计，并对多个前沿 MLLM 进行实证评估。

Result: 实验发现明显的"空间鸿沟"：结构化 GeoJSON 持续优于地图图像，多模态组合相较单独 GeoJSON 无稳定增益；定性拓扑与顺序关系表现尚可，但定量度量关系性能明显下降；顺序关系上的编辑性能较 QA 下降，且多数失败编辑至少违反一项指定空间约束。

Conclusion: GeoSpatialBench 为诊断 MLLM 空间能力提供了开放、严谨的测试平台，揭示被动识别空间关系与主动操控几何配置之间的能力缺口，为物理落地的世界模型与具身空间 AI 提供关键实证基础。

Abstract: Understanding and manipulating spatial relations is fundamental to AI systems that reason about physical environments and construct grounded world models. However, existing evaluations of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for spatial reasoning rely predominantly on static question-answering (QA) over small-scale or synthetic scenes, rarely probing whether models can modify spatial configurations while satisfying explicit geometric constraints. We introduce GeoSpatialBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate an escalating progression of spatial cognition, ranging from spatial relation understanding and geometric reasoning to constrained spatial editing. Grounded in real-world urban data across six geographically diverse cities, GeoSpatialBench evaluates a comprehensive taxonomy of 28 topological, directional, metric, and ordering relations across structured GeoJSON and map image modalities, with ground-truth references and constraints verified via GIS operations. Our empirical evaluation across frontier MLLMs uncovers a stark spatial gap: structured GeoJSON consistently outperforms map images, with multimodal combinations offering no reliable gain over GeoJSON alone, and while models show reasonable competence on qualitative topological and ordering, performance drops on quantitative metric relations. Crucially, editing performance on ordering relations declines relative to QA, and most failed edits violate at least one specified spatial constraint. These results highlight a gap between passively recognizing spatial relations and actively manipulating geometric configurations. GeoSpatialBench provides an open, rigorous diagnostic testbed to benchmark these spatial limitations, laying essential empirical groundwork for physically grounded world models and embodied spatial AI.

</details>


### [98] [OGPO: Offline Goal-conditioned Policy Optimization for Recoverable Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12914)
*Xule Gao,Xi Wang,Zehua Zang,Rui Wang,Changwen Zheng,Chuxiong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OGPO，一种面向VLA模型后训练的可恢复离线目标条件策略优化框架，将轨迹级动作模仿转为关键状态可达性学习，在LIBERO与MetaWorld上稳定优于监督微调，并在扰动基准上展现更强鲁棒性与恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型后训练主要依赖对示教轨迹的轨迹级监督模仿。该方法虽能学到可行动作，但逐步复现专家动作会使策略偏向示教特定的动作路径；一旦执行偏离示教轨迹，缺乏引导策略回到任务进展状态的信号，恢复能力不足。

Method: OGPO不把离线示教仅当作逐步动作标签，而是用语义关键状态对轨迹进行自适应重标注，并构建目标条件过程奖励，优化策略以到达这些任务进展状态。由此将后训练从轨迹级动作模仿转变为关键状态可达性学习，无需额外在线交互即可让策略具备从可行但偏离示教的states完成目标状态的能力。

Result: 在LIBERO和MetaWorld上，OGPO相较标准监督微调持续提升VLA策略性能。在LIBERO-Plus以及作者提出的LIBERO-ReAct（执行中扰动基准）上，OGPO在分布偏移与可恢复的偏离示教状态下表现出更强的鲁棒性与目标导向恢复能力。

Conclusion: OGPO通过关键状态重标注与目标条件过程奖励，将VLA后训练重塑为关键状态可达性学习，在不增加在线交互的前提下提升了策略性能、分布偏移鲁棒性与执行偏离后的恢复能力，为可恢复的VLA后训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown strong promise in robotic manipulation, yet their post-training still predominantly relies on trajectory-level supervised imitation over robot demonstrations. While effective for learning feasible actions, this paradigm supervises policies to reproduce expert actions at each step, biasing them toward demonstration-specific action paths and providing limited guidance for recovering toward task-progress states once execution deviates from demonstrated trajectories. To address this limitation, we propose OGPO, an Offline Goal-conditioned Policy Optimization framework for recoverable VLA post-training. Instead of treating offline demonstrations merely as step-wise action labels, OGPO adaptively relabels trajectories with semantic key states and constructs goal-conditioned process rewards to optimize the policy for reaching these task-progress states. In this way, OGPO shifts VLA post-training from trajectory-level action imitation to key-state reachability learning, enabling policies to acquire a more robust ability to complete goal states from feasible off-demonstration states without requiring additional online interaction. Experiments on LIBERO and MetaWorld show that OGPO consistently improves VLA policy performance over standard supervised fine-tuning. Moreover, evaluations on LIBERO-Plus and our proposed LIBERO-ReAct, a mid-execution perturbation benchmark, further demonstrate that OGPO achieves stronger robustness and goal-directed recoverability under distribution shifts and recoverable off-demonstration states.

</details>


### [99] [DeFiRank: Federated Pairwise Ranking for Governance in Decentralized Financial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12916)
*Zahra Batool,Michael Duchesne,Kaiwen Zhang,Ulrich Aivodji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于联邦学习的成对排序框架，用于去中心化金融网络的治理评级，在保护数据隐私的前提下实现接近集中式基线的排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化金融网络的治理依赖人工委员会审查，存在主观偏见、高网络负载下的扩展性瓶颈，且难以捕捉复杂行为模式与动态演化的网络结构。

Method: 采用联邦学习驱动的成对排序（pairwise ranking）框架，多家机构在不共享私有交易数据的前提下协同训练排序模型；模型利用网络级行为特征（交易对手多样性、多应用使用、交易频次等）评估提供者的行为真实性，并据此提升真实贡献者、降低可疑行为者。

Result: 在真实区块链交易数据集上，联邦方法排序性能略低于集中式基线（NDCG@15 约 0.87 vs 约 0.92），排序稳定性相当（Kendall τ 均约 0.70），并产生可靠的治理决策：648 个提供者被提升、39 个被降级，且无需集中访问敏感交易数据。

Conclusion: 联邦成对排序可在不集中敏感数据的情况下支撑去中心化金融网络的治理决策，在隐私保护与排序质量之间取得有效折中。

Abstract: Existing governance approaches in decentralized financial networks rely on manual review processes conducted by human committees, which introduces subjective bias, creates a scalability bottleneck under high network volume, and fails to capture complex behavioural patterns and evolving network dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose \name, a federated learning-based pairwise ranking framework in which multiple institutions collaboratively train a ranking model without sharing their private transaction data. This collaborative learning enables the model to capture network-wide behavioural patterns that signify strong network participation and directly contribute to the network effect, including diversity of counterparties, usage of multiple applications, and frequency of transactions. The proposed system ranks providers based on the authenticity of their actions, promoting genuine contributors and demoting those exhibiting suspicious behaviour. We evaluate \name\ on a real-world blockchain transaction dataset. Results demonstrate that the federated approach achieves ranking performance modestly below the centralized baseline (NDCG@15: $\approx$0.87 vs.\ $\approx$0.92) while preserving data privacy and matching its ranking stability (Kendall $τ$: $\approx$0.70 for both regimes), producing reliable governance decisions, including provider promotion and demotion (648 and 39 unique providers, respectively), without requiring centralized access to sensitive transaction data.

</details>


### [100] [AI-Mediation in Retail Markets: Revenue Effects and Assortment Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12917)
*Omar El Housni,Marouane Ibn Brahim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究AI购物助手介入下的零售品类规划，构建AI排序数据并估计Plackett-Luce模型；发现忽略AI中介会造成2.52%–11.37%收入损失，而基于拟合AI效用排序的确定性插件表现更优（损失≤0.53%），并给出确定性中介下品类优化的复杂度与FPTAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI购物助手日益影响消费者考虑哪些产品，中介机制会改变零售商最优品类与收入，但现有品类规划未系统纳入AI中介。论文旨在量化其影响并求解对应优化问题。

Method: 建立三层框架：零售商选目录、AI形成面向客户的候选清单、客户按多项Logit选择。考虑两类中介：确定性中介（固定分段排序）与基于Plackett-Luce的随机中介。实证上构建领先AI模型的产品排序数据集，估计PL排序模型并在留出品集评估预测；结合合成客户偏好比较不同优化策略的收入；进一步分析确定性中介下品类优化的计算复杂度并设计近似算法。

Result: 跨测试容量配置，忽略中介相对PL最优平均收入损失为2.52%–11.37%；仅基于拟合AI效用排序的确定性插件平均损失至多0.53%。对确定性中介品类优化，刻画其计算复杂度；当客户分段数固定时，问题存在全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS）。

Conclusion: AI中介显著影响最优品类与收益，简单确定性插件即可接近PL最优；确定性中介下的品类优化虽计算困难，但在固定分段数下可由FPTAS有效近似，为零售品类规划提供可操作方案。

Abstract: AI shopping assistants increasingly influence which products consumers consider. We study retail assortment planning under AI mediation, where a retailer chooses a catalog, an AI intermediary forms a customer-facing shortlist, and the customer chooses from that shortlist according to a multinomial logit model. We consider both deterministic mediation, in which the AI follows a fixed segment-specific ranking, and stochastic mediation based on the Plackett-Luce model.
  Empirically, we construct a dataset of product rankings from leading AI models, estimate Plackett-Luce ranking models, and evaluate their predictive performance on held-out assortments. Combining these estimates with synthetic customer preferences, we find that accounting for AI mediation can materially change optimal assortments and revenue. Across the tested capacity configurations, ignoring mediation yields mean revenue losses between 2.52% and 11.37% relative to the PL optimum. A much simpler deterministic plug-in, based only on the ordering of fitted AI utilities, performs substantially better, with mean losses of at most 0.53%.
  Motivated by this performance, we study assortment optimization under deterministic mediation. We characterize its computational complexity and show that, for any fixed number of customer segments, the problem admits a fully polynomial-time approximation scheme.

</details>


### [101] [Teaching neural decoders with language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12921)
*Xuan Wen,Yonghao Song,Yuhong Zhang,Guoliang Li,Tzyy-Ping Jung,Xiaorong Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"语言即监督"(LaS),用自然语言类别描述对齐神经特征,以文本原型做分类,在多任务、多编码器上优于类别标签监督(CLS)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统类别标签监督(CLS)仅提供离散标签,类别间关系被隐式化,缺乏语义结构与可迁移的参照。

Method: LaS通过可学习投影将神经特征与自然语言类别描述对齐,并用候选文本原型进行类别预测;在视觉、运动、言语解码任务上评估七种编码器架构,覆盖头皮与颅内记录,且各模型独立训练。

Result: 言语解码准确率较匹配的CLS基线最高提升17.15个百分点;表征支持候选集复用及标签监督的受试者与会话适配;可实现SSVEP中条件化的未见频率解码;正确类别描述对应关系是泛化关键;双侧归因可视化显示神经与文本对同一类别匹配分数的贡献。

Conclusion: 文本描述可作为神经解码中学习与解释的通用参照。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces decode neural activity, yet class-label supervision (CLS) leaves relationships among classes implicit. We propose Language as Supervision (LaS), which aligns neural features with natural-language class descriptions through learned projections and predicts classes using candidate text prototypes. We evaluate seven encoder architectures with independently trained models for visual, motor, and speech decoding from scalp and intracranial recordings. LaS improves speech-decoding accuracy by up to 17.15~pp over matched CLS baselines. Its representations support candidate-set reuse and label-supervised subject and session adaptation, while LaS enables conditional unseen-frequency decoding in steady-state visual evoked potential (SSVEP) responses. Correct class-description correspondence supports this generalization, and dual-sided attribution visualization shows neural and textual contributions to the same class-matching score. These findings suggest that text descriptions can serve as general-purpose references for learning and interpretation in neural decoding.

</details>


### [102] [Collaborative Learning and Reasoning over Model Slices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12927)
*Yuheng Wang,Runde Yang,Zhuohan Bao,Qiran Zhang,Huatao Li,Yiyang Zheng,Lin Wu,Jingru Fan,Shu Yao,Yihan Li,Zhi Li,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"协作学习"框架，通过模型切片构建异构协作者并用金字塔监督训练跨模型协作，使多个小模型在可比参数预算下达到或超越原大模型及多智能体基线，同时推理效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单模型推理存在错误传播问题：后续推理步骤依赖模型此前的输出，导致推理盲点难以被独立复核与修正。作者希望借助模型异构性来重新审视前期推理，从而缓解单模型的固有脆弱性。

Method: 方法包含两部分：(1) 模型切片（model slicing），用多个异构小模型在相近参数预算下替代单个大模型，使先前推理能在不同参数化下被重新评估；(2) 金字塔监督（Pyramid supervision），结合协议蒸馏与步骤级监督，优化协作过程中的推理与交互决策。

Result: 在四类任务上评估：多跳问答、数学推理、代码生成和开放约束生成。训练后的异构模型任务表现与原大模型及现有多智能体基线（含强化学习方法）相当或更优，推理效率高于多智能体基线，并在不同任务上呈现差异化的协作模式。

Conclusion: 学习到的协作能把模型异构性转化为互补的推理能力，既缓解单模型错误传播，又兼顾性能与推理效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel across diverse tasks, benefiting from knowledge priors, in-context reasoning, and test-time computation. Despite these strengths, single-model reasoning remains vulnerable to error propagation, as later steps depend on the model's earlier outputs, leaving reasoning blind spots difficult to independently reassess. To address this limitation, we develop \textit{collaborative learning}, encompassing the construction of heterogeneous collaborators and the learning of effective cross-model collaboration. Specifically, \textit{model slicing} replaces a large model with smaller, heterogeneous models under a comparable parameter budget, enabling prior reasoning to be reassessed across distinct model parameterizations. \textit{Pyramid supervision} combines protocol distillation with step-level supervision to refine reasoning and interaction decisions throughout collaboration. We evaluate our approach across four task categories: multi-hop question answering, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and open-ended constrained generation. The trained heterogeneous models achieve comparable or better task performance than the original large model and established multi-agent baselines, including reinforcement-learning-based methods, while also achieving higher inference efficiency than the multi-agent baselines and exhibiting differentiated collaboration patterns across tasks. These findings show that learned collaboration turns model heterogeneity into complementary reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [103] [Learning More by Training Less: Uncertainty-Guided Progressive Contraction for Efficient Reasoning Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12933)
*Suorong Yang,Jiaxin Wang,Jiaming Su,Chentao Yue,Furao Shen,Peng Ye,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"不确定性引导的渐进收缩"框架，通过对推理监督进行压缩，仅用全量SFT约0.22%的优化预算（训练曝光减少450倍以上）即可维持甚至提升推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用越来越大的语料扩展推理后训练会显著增加训练成本，但性能收益并不成比例。作者据此提出核心问题：在不牺牲能力的前提下，推理监督能被压缩到何种程度？

Method: 不将教师生成的思维链（CoT）视为单一实现路径，而是围绕中间推理状态构建蒙特卡洛推理树，暴露过程级不确定性，形成带不确定性画像的推理监督。该不确定性首先将原始语料收缩为紧凑的、教师指导的池子；随后的学习者感知收缩在SFT过程中进行在线路由，依据学生不断演变的学习状态集中优化。两阶段共同渐进收缩监督与优化，大幅降低学生训练预算。

Result: 在多种推理基准与多类LLM家族上，方法仅使用全量数据SFT约0.22%的优化预算、训练曝光减少450倍以上，仍能维持或提升推理性能。框架在异构学生家族、黑盒教师以及弱到强迁移上均具有良好泛化性。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅是加速训练的手段，还能增强推理能力，表明推理监督可在极大压缩下仍保持并提升模型能力。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable advances in complex reasoning. However, scaling reasoning post-training with ever-larger corpora can substantially increase training cost without proportional performance gains. In this study, we therefore ask: how far can reasoning supervision be compressed without sacrificing capability?
