In this book we will discuss how agentic LLMs work, how they act, how they interact, and what this means for us, as users.

</details>


### [137] [SqueezeBench: Saturation-Resilient Evaluation of Mathematical Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13461)
*Pablo Manrique,Hai Xia,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SqueezeBench——一个抗饱和的数学基准。每个问题固定一个仅在小子数 n 上已知取值的整数函数 f(n)，基准维护其可计算 Lean 函数的下界与上界记录，提交需机器可验证地证明新函数从两侧夹逼 f、不劣于当前记录且在某处严格更优，从而持续抬高门槛而非删除问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 在数学上的进展难以度量：已解问题基准趋于饱和，而开放问题基准中每个问题只能被首次解决一次，无法持续提供难度信号。需要一种不因被解决而失效、能反复衡量进展的基准形式。

Method: 构建记录式（record-based）基准：对每个问题固定整数函数 f(n)，维护一个下界记录与一个上界记录，二者均为带机器验证证明的 Lean 可计算函数。提交需给出新函数，并证明其界住 f、在所有 n 上不劣于当前记录、且在某 n 上严格更优；通过后成为下一提交需超越的新记录。系统同时评估界的取值与证明，允许在单点或增长率上改进。所有证明由两个独立的 Lean 内核检查，无需人工审稿。检查会拒绝通过小子数 n 穷举搜索来计算 f 的函数，维护者仅阅读提交报告并排查更大 n 上的此类搜索。

Result: 发布来自极值组合学的十个问题，含二十个已证明的初始记录与准入工具。作为基线，基于前沿语言模型的智能体（每个记录至多一小时）被接受 82 份提交并改进了所有记录，且未超出已发表文献范围。基于两个更新模型的智能体从这些记录出发，又有 60 份提交被接受并再次改进 20 个记录中的 19 个。十个问题中有八个至少有一个记录仍在渐近意义上落后于最佳已发表界。

Conclusion: SqueezeBench 通过"成功即抬高门槛"的记录机制实现抗饱和评估，并借助双内核 Lean 验证实现全自动、无需裁判的数学正确性检查。实验表明该基准可持续产生改进信号、支持智能体迭代超越，且多数问题仍留有超越已发表界的空间，具备长期区分度。

Abstract: AI systems are making rapid progress in mathematics, but this progress is hard to measure. Benchmarks of solved problems saturate, and in benchmarks of open problems each problem can be resolved for the first time only once. We present SqueezeBench, a benchmark that is resilient to saturation: a success raises the bar instead of removing the problem. Each problem fixes an integer function $f(n)$ whose values are known only for small $n$, and the benchmark keeps a lower and an upper record on it. A record is a computable Lean function with a machine-checked proof that it bounds $f$. A submission proposes a new function and proves that it bounds $f$, that it is no worse than the current record at any $n$, and that it is strictly better at some $n$. Once accepted, it becomes the record that the next submission has to beat. The system thus chooses the bound as well as its proof, and it may improve the record at a single $n$ or its growth rate. At every $n$, the gap between the two records shows how far the certified bounds are from determining $f(n)$. Every proof is checked by two independent Lean kernels, so no referee needs to check the mathematics. The checks also reject a function that computes $f$ by exhaustive search at small $n$, and a maintainer only reads the submitter's report and looks for such a search at larger $n$. We release ten problems from extremal combinatorics with twenty proved initial records and the admission tools. As a baseline, agents based on a frontier language model, with at most one hour per record, had 82 submissions accepted and improved every record, without going beyond the published literature. Agents based on two newer models then started from these records, had 60 more submissions accepted and improved 19 of the 20 records again. For eight of the ten problems, at least one record is still asymptotically behind the best published bound.

</details>


### [138] [A Local Agentic AI Framework to Generate Edge Case Scenarios for Safety Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13479)
*Victoria J. Hodge,Colin Paterson,Rob Alexander*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于本地语言模型的智能体式AI框架，用于自动化生成自动驾驶等自主系统的边缘场景，通过仿真指标反馈驱动探索与利用，并生成可追溯的安全保障证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘场景生成对自主系统的训练、测试与安全保证至关重要，可识别危险失效、提升可靠性与鲁棒性并覆盖运行设计域边界。现有语言模型场景生成方法多依赖人工介入或规则策略，限制了生成多样性与边缘案例（长尾分布）的能力。

Method: 采用笔记本电脑上的本地LMs构建智能体AI框架，通过think、act、observe、update、continue循环与轻量JSON编码生成场景；以系统需求、系统描述和环境描述作为上下文提示的知识库保证场景真实性；由第二个独立LM按领域特定标准对每个场景进行评审与精炼；精炼后的场景在仿真中运行并采集评估指标；根据指标分析进行利用（生成相似边缘场景）或探索（生成新场景）；最后通过基于指标分数的聚类与富文本场景描述的主题类型分析定位功能缺口及其触发条件。

Result: 该框架实现了自适应场景生成，成本低、完全可控并保障数据隐私与安全；通过仿真指标反馈驱动探索与利用；事后聚类与主题分析可识别功能缺口及其触发条件，形成可追溯的安全保障证据。

Conclusion: 研究展示了灵活且自适应的本地智能体AI框架在自动化边缘场景生成和产出可追溯安全保障证据方面的潜力。

Abstract: Generating edge case scenarios is crucial for training, testing and safety assuring autonomous systems. This identifies hazardous failures to increase system reliability and robustness, ensures the system can still function safely on the boundaries of its operational design domain and learns the expected system behaviour for these edge cases. The rapid advancement in the generation and analysis capabilities of language models (LMs) has expanded their applicability and recent work has investigated LM scenario generation. However, many recent approaches rely on human-in-the-loop or rule-based strategies. This restricts the LM's ability to generate diverse and edge case scenarios; the long tail of the distribution. Our work uses local LMs in a laptop-based agentic AI framework for adaptive scenario generation. This is cheap, fully-controlled, ensures data privacy and security. It uses think, act, observe, update, and continue loops with lightweight JSON encoding to generate scenarios. We use the system requirements, system description and environment description as knowledge bases in contextual prompts for an LM to ensure scenario realism. A second, independent LM critiques each scenario using domain specific criteria so it can be refined. The refined scenarios are run in simulation and evaluation metrics collected. These metric scores are analysed and similar scenarios are created for edge cases (exploitation) or novel scenarios are generated to explore the scenario space. Post-hoc clustering using metric scores and thematic-type analysis of the rich-text scenario descriptions produced by the LM pinpoints functional gaps and their triggering conditions. This creates traceable evidence for the safety assurance case. Our work highlights the potential of a flexible and adaptive local agentic AI framework for automating edge case generation and producing traceable safety assurance evidence.

</details>


### [139] [Branching On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13481)
*Dongyang Chen,Shudong Liu,Shubo Li,Duoxun Tang,Jinwei Liang,Zehuan Wu,Lewei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 BranchOPD，利用学生不确定性与师生分歧选择决策点、从共享前缀重采样分支，并对主轨迹与分支施加 token 级教师监督；配合协议边界修复（PBR），在数学与智能体任务上超越 Vanilla OPD 与多轨迹 OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy 蒸馏（OPD）虽能在学生自生成轨迹上提供教师监督，但单条轨迹在每个决策点只实现一种后续，采样预算有限时监督覆盖不足。

Method: 用学生不确定性 + 师生分歧挑选信息量大的决策点；从共享前缀重采样学生策略后续形成分支；对主轨迹和分支统一施加 token 级教师监督；数学任务在 token 级决策、智能体任务在回合内决策上操作；共享前缀复用降低学生生成 token 成本；并提出 protocol-boundary repair（PBR），在协议约束下做定向监督以缓解重复与无效生成。

Result: 相比 7 条独立轨迹，共享前缀复用使 ALFWorld 生成 token 成本降约 19%、WebShop 降约 31%；在全部评测基准上优于 Vanilla OPD 与 Multi-Rollout OPD(n=7)；数学 avg@16 平均准确率较 Vanilla OPD 提升 18.55 个百分点，ALFWorld ID/OOD 平均成功率提升 13.8 点；机制分析与选择消融显示，在不确定决策点分支可扩展后续行为覆盖，并优于随机选择与回合初始分支。

Conclusion: 在不确定/分歧决策点进行共享前缀分支，是提升 on-policy 蒸馏监督覆盖与效率的有效策略，配合 PBR 可进一步抑制重复与浪费生成。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides teacher supervision on student-generated trajectories, but a single trajectory realizes only one continuation at each decision, limiting supervision coverage under a finite sampling budget. We propose BranchOPD (Branching On-Policy Distillation), which selects informative decision points using student uncertainty and teacher-student disagreement, resamples student-policy continuations from shared prefixes, and applies token-level teacher supervision to both main trajectories and branches. BranchOPD operates at token-level decisions in mathematics and within-turn decisions in agent tasks. Shared-prefix reuse reduces student-generated token costs by approximately 19% on ALFWorld and 31% on WebShop relative to seven independent trajectories. We also introduce protocol-boundary repair (PBR), which uses targeted supervision under protocol constraints to mitigate repetition and wasted generation. BranchOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD and Multi-Rollout OPD (n=7) across all evaluated benchmarks, with gains over Vanilla OPD of 18.55 percentage points in mean mathematics accuracy over 16 sampled answers (avg@16) and 13.8 points in mean ALFWorld ID/OOD success. Mechanistic analyses and selection ablations show that branching at uncertain decisions expands subsequent behavior coverage and outperforms random selection and turn-initial branching.

</details>


### [140] [Humanize MLE Agents: Multi-Agent Session Alternation Beyond Single-Agent Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13494)
*Changye Li,Zijian Zhang,Muheng Li,Yiduo Pang,Tingjun Wu,Menghan Li,Yitong Liu,Hongkai Guo,Mengchi Chen,Chenyu He,Kaisheng Ma,Wei Xu,Song Han,Sihao Liu,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发现LLM智能体在长周期MLE任务中存在"长周期减速"与"工具架陈旧"问题，提出以会话交替为核心的工作流级方案HMA，在MLE-bench上取得78.2%的any-medal率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体在长周期机器学习工程中依赖上下文工程（通过定制化工具架管理工具、技能与积累经验），但存在两个问题：单会话执行越长，推理强度下降、进展变慢（长周期减速）；且随着底层模型升级，定制工具架的收益消失（工具架陈旧）。这表明这类收益难以跨版本维持，因此需要转向跨会话、智能体相互接力的工作流级方法。

Method: 作者将交替进行的智能体会话建模为"上下文重置半马尔可夫决策过程（CR-SMDP）"，给出交替优于单智能体参考上限的充分条件，推导出显式的实验预算界，并刻画会话上限在何种条件下能保持该优势。基于此分析提出HMA（Humanize MLE Agents）：在共享工作空间上交替运行两个智能体，每个会话最多进行k次实验，会话之间重置上下文，且不额外添加任务特定工具架或先验知识补充。

Result: 在MLE-bench的75个Kaggle任务上，HMA达到78.2%的any-medal率（当前最优），比最强开源基线高8.0个百分点，且仅用其四分之一的时间预算。在覆盖五组异构智能体配对的六种配置中，HMA相比各组成基线平均提升6.4个百分点，体现出跨配对通用性。补充的kernel优化案例达到878个cycle，距社区排行榜首位仅差1.6%，说明其适用性不限于MLE。

Conclusion: 研究表明，长周期智能体的性能瓶颈来自单会话内的推理衰减与定制工具架的版本依赖，而通过上下文重置与多智能体会话交替的工作流级设计，可在不依赖任务特定工具架的情况下显著提升长周期MLE表现，并具备跨配对与跨任务的通用性。

Abstract: Existing LLM-based agent approaches for long-horizon machine learning engineering (MLE) often use context engineering, implemented through custom harnesses that manage tools, skills, and accumulated experience. However, we find that as single-session execution lengthens, the agent's reasoning intensity declines alongside slowing progress, summarized as long-horizon deceleration, and the benefits of these custom harnesses vanish as the underlying model improves, summarized as harness staleness. These findings suggest that their benefits may not persist across versions, which motivates a workflow-level approach in which agents build on one another's work across sessions. We model these alternating agent sessions as a context-reset semi-Markov decision process (CR-SMDP) and establish sufficient conditions for alternation to exceed single-agent reference limits, derive an explicit experiment-budget bound, and characterize when session caps preserve this advantage. Guided by this analysis, we introduce Humanize MLE Agents (HMA), which alternates two agents on a shared workspace, limits each session to at most k experiments and resets the context between sessions, without additional task-specific harness or prior knowledge supplements. Across 75 Kaggle tasks in MLE-bench, HMA reaches a state-of-the-art any-medal rate of 78.2%, exceeding the strongest open-source baseline by 8.0 points with a quarter of its time budget. Moreover, among six configurations spanning five heterogeneous agent pairs, HMA improves any-medal rate over the constituent baselines by 6.4 points on average, demonstrating cross-pair generality. A supplementary case on kernel optimization reaches 878 cycles, within 1.6% of the community leaderboard leader, demonstrating applicability beyond MLE.

</details>


### [141] [Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Language Model with Heterogeneous Evidence Integration for Clinical Prognosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13510)
*Hamza Shafiq,Jialu Tang,Ting Dang,Jun Hu,Aaqib Saeed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRISM 是一个融合结构化测量、生理波形、纵向病史与人群背景的多模态语言模型，在 MDS-ED 基准上诊断/恶化/ICU 入住/死亡预测 AUROC 分别达 86.2%/93.1%/91.8%/92.8%，并通过消融与外部迁移验证了各模态组分的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预后预测需要同时整合结构化测量、生理波形、纵向病史与人群背景等多源异质信息；现有基线方法（如 UniPACT、MDS-ED）在输入窗口与多模态融合效率上存在不足，缺乏结构感知的融合与人群检索机制。

Method: 提出 PRISM（Population-Retrieved Inference for Structured Multimodal Prognosis）：(1) 逐特征数值分词（numerical tokenization）；(2) 对纵向 ECG 做近因加权聚合（recency-weighted aggregation）；(3) 双模态人群记忆库（dual-modality population memory bank），检索在表示空间进行，仅将相似训练队列 encounter 的均值池化嵌入输入模型，不把单条检索记录写入 prompt。评估使用 1,443 任务的 MDS-ED 基准，并设置匹配输入的紧凑融合基线、数值编码替换、历史移除、检索置零及随机邻居检索等消融。

Result: PRISM 在诊断/恶化/ICU 入住/死亡预测上 AUROC 为 86.2%/93.1%/91.8%/92.8%（使用基线不可得的 96 小时到达前窗口）。仅用基准窗口输入时 PRISM 0.8895，UniPACT 0.8937，MDS-ED 0.8889；使用相同五路证据与冻结编码器的匹配输入紧凑融合模型仅 0.7652，PRISM 为 0.9092。将数值 EHR 编码改为文本序列化使 AUROC 下降 3.66 点，移除到达前病史下降 1.97 点，检索置零下降 0.99 点。真实检索较随机邻居检索高 0.97 宏平均点，但配对任务级符号检验未确立一致性优势（p=0.067）。外部 MC-MED 队列上，有监督微调达 0.8611，优于本地训练梯度提升的 0.8227 与仅源域 PRISM 的 0.7841。

Conclusion: 结果支持结构感知的多模态融合与有监督外部适配策略：数值分词、纵向历史与人群检索均带来增益，检索优势虽未达统计显著但方向一致；外部有监督微调显著优于本地基线与仅源域模型，表明该框架具备跨队列迁移潜力。

Abstract: Clinical prognosis requires integrating structured measurements, physiological waveforms, longitudinal history, and population context. We introduce PRISM (Population-Retrieved Inference for Structured Multimodal Prognosis), a multimodal language model that combines per-feature numerical tokenization, recency-weighted aggregation of longitudinal ECGs, and a dual-modality population memory bank. Retrieval operates in representation space: the model receives mean-pooled embeddings of similar training-cohort encounters, while no individual retrieved record enters the prompt. On the 1,443-task MDS-ED benchmark, PRISM achieves AUROCs of 86.2% for diagnosis, 93.1% for deterioration, 91.8% for ICU admission, and 92.8% for mortality. These results use a 96-hour pre-arrival window unavailable to reported baselines. With benchmark-window inputs only, PRISM reaches 0.8895 AUROC, compared with 0.8937 for UniPACT and 0.8889 for MDS-ED. A matched-input compact fusion model using the same five evidence streams and frozen encoders reaches 0.7652 AUROC, versus 0.9092 for PRISM. Replacing numerical EHR encoding with text serialization reduces AUROC by 3.66 points; removing pre-arrival history reduces it by 1.97 points; and zeroing retrieval reduces it by 0.99 points. Genuine retrieval outperforms random-neighbor retrieval by 0.97 macro-average points, although a paired task-level sign test does not establish a uniform advantage (p=0.067). On the external MC-MED cohort, supervised fine-tuning reaches 0.8611 AUROC, compared with 0.8227 for locally trained gradient boosting and 0.7841 for source-only PRISM. These results support structure-aware multimodal integration and supervised external adaptation.

</details>


### [142] [Causal Effect Identifiability in Causal Abstractions: A Strict Hierarchy of Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13520)
*Clément Yvernes,Marianne Clausel,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将因果效应可识别性从单个因果图提升到因果抽象（图集合）层面，形式化若干可识别性概念并构建层级关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果效应识别需完整指定的因果图，在复杂或高维场景中该假设常不现实。因果抽象通过保留部分因果信息、定义相容因果图的等价类来缓解此问题，但既有文献在图集合层面的可识别性定义存在缺口与歧义。

Method: 不针对单个因果图，而是直接在因果抽象所对应的图集合层面研究可识别性：形式化该设定下的多种可识别性概念，并将其组织成一个层级结构以厘清彼此关系与差异。

Result: 建立了多种可识别性概念之间的层级关系；特别证明：因果效应可在整个图集合上被识别，却不必然存在一条对所有相容图一致成立的单一因果推导。

Conclusion: 在图集合层面研究可识别性可揭示现有文献中的空白并澄清歧义，为因果抽象下的因果效应识别提供了更细致的概念框架。

Abstract: Identifying causal effects from observational data typically requires a fully specified causal graph, an assumption that is often unrealistic in complex or high-dimensional settings. Causal abstractions address this limitation by retaining only partial causal information, thereby defining equivalence classes of compatible causal graphs. In this paper, we study causal effect identifiability directly at the level of these graph collections, rather than at the level of individual causal diagrams. We formalize several notions of identifiability in this setting and organize them into a hierarchy that clarifies their relationships and differences. In particular, we establish that a causal effect may be identified over a collection of graphs without admitting a single causal derivation that holds uniformly across all compatible graphs. These results help reveal gaps and clarify ambiguities in the existing literature.

</details>


### [143] [Beyond Domain-Routed Supervision: Cross-Teacher Guarding for Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13521)
*Shi-Yu Tian,Yong Liu,Ximan Liu,Tianrui Zhu,Sule Bai,Xinwei Yang,Ruiqi Liu,Kun-Yang Yu,Ming Yang,Zhi Zhou,Yu-Feng Li,Yipeng Sun,Gu Simiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Road MOPD，通过跨教师守护机制解决多教师在线蒸馏中的梯度冲突与能力退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师在线蒸馏（MOPD）将领域特定数据路由至专门教师，在共享参数上进行密集token级监督。但领域路由只区分监督来源，所有领域的更新仍作用于共享参数；当教师在响应风格与领域专长上差异较大时，单独有效的监督在联合优化中会产生持续的梯度冲突与能力退化。

Method: 提出Cross-Teacher Guarding for MOPD（Road MOPD）：引入非路由教师作为同一学生轨迹上的跨域辅助评论者，通过token级信用分配与梯度更新协调多教师监督。具体包括：(1) 跨教师引导的信用分配，利用教师一致性增强被教师共同支持的token级纠正、衰减冲突信号；(2) 预防性多教师梯度投影，用同一轨迹上的辅助梯度约束潜在冲突更新，并自适应控制投影强度以保留目标领域学习信号。

Result: 实验中，Road MOPD缓解了劣势领域的能力退化，在高冲突场景下提升超过5个百分点，并在低冲突与三领域设置下改善了整体性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入跨域辅助评论者并协调token级信用分配与梯度投影，Road MOPD有效缓解了MOPD中共享参数上的梯度冲突，在多种冲突程度与领域数量设置下均取得性能提升。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) extends on-policy distillation (OPD) by routing domain-specific data to specialized teachers, integrating their capabilities through dense token-level supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, domain routing only distinguishes the supervision sources for different domains; updates from all domains still act on shared parameters. When teachers differ substantially in response style and domain expertise, supervision that is effective in isolation may still lead to persistent gradient conflicts and capability degradation during joint optimization. To address this challenge, we propose Cross-Teacher Guarding for MOPD (Road MOPD), which introduces non-routed teachers as auxiliary cross-domain critics on the same student trajectories and coordinates multi-teacher supervision through token-level credit assignment and gradient updates. Specifically, cross-teacher-guided credit assignment uses teacher agreement to strengthen token-level corrections jointly supported by the teachers and attenuate conflicting signals; preventive multi-teacher gradient projection uses auxiliary gradients from the same trajectories to constrain potentially conflicting updates and adaptively controls projection strength to retain target-domain learning signals. In experiments, Road MOPD mitigates degradation in the disadvantaged domain, gains over 5 percentage points under high conflict, and improves overall performance in low-conflict and three-domain settings.

</details>


### [144] [MAGMA-Bench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Embodied Agents under Dynamic Tasks and Execution Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13534)
*Loan Bernat,Matthieu Grard,Ariane Herbulot,Florent Lamiraux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGMA-Bench 是一个面向动态多机器人环境的架构无关基准，用于评估长时程持续任务推理能力，涵盖异构机器人协作与在线扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准分别研究动态任务执行与具身多智能体协作，但对二者交叉领域的评估不足。长时程具身智能体需维持任务相关信息、协调异构机器人并随执行过程动态调整决策。

Method: 构建 MAGMA-Bench 基准，采用结构化技能级接口，将任务级推理与感知及底层控制解耦；通过递增任务时程与受控在线扰动（执行失败、任务中断、任务更新）进行评估，场景要求具备异构能力的机器人间协作；并评估本地可部署的开源权重规划器、多智能体状态跟踪策略及前沿模型。

Result: 即使在无扰动的干净回合中，最强模型也有相当比例失败，在线扰动下成功率进一步下降；监督式适配能显著提升小型模型，但在更长时程及多机器人任务上性能仍明显偏低。

Conclusion: MAGMA-Bench 揭示当前模型在动态多机器人长时程任务推理上的显著不足，并开源基准、261,646 条任务级训练样本及架构无关的专家轨迹生成流水线，以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied agents must maintain task-relevant information, coordinate heterogeneous robots, and adapt their decisions as execution unfolds. Existing benchmarks investigate dynamic task execution and embodied multi-agent coordination, but provide limited evaluation of their intersection. We introduce MAGMA-Bench, an architecture-agnostic benchmark for persistent task reasoning in dynamic multi-robot environments. MAGMA-Bench evaluates agents across increasing task horizons and controlled online perturbations, including execution failures, task interruptions, and mission updates, with scenarios requiring coordination across robots with heterogeneous capabilities. It uses a structured skill-level interface to separate task-level reasoning from perception and low-level control. We evaluate locally deployable open-weight planners, multi-agent state-tracking strategies, and frontier models. Even the strongest evaluated models fail on a substantial fraction of clean episodes, and their success rates decrease further under online perturbations. Supervised adaptation greatly improves a small model, but performance remains substantially lower on longer and multi-robot missions. We release the benchmark, 261,646 task-level training examples, and an architecture-agnostic expert-trace generation pipeline.

</details>


### [145] [Constructing Pareto Frontiers of Optimal Reasoning Termination Methods for Overthinking in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13570)
*Weixin Guan,Liang Li,Shiyu Xie,Jiapeng Liu,Bing Li,Peng Fu,Chengyang Fang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Can Ma,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出Step-wise Truncation Probing（STP）方法，在模型-任务层面刻画推理模型的过度思考（overthinking）现象，并统一量化不同终止策略的理论缓解潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型可受益于测试时扩展（test-time scaling），但持续推理可能无效甚至有害，即"过度思考"。现有评估仅对比完整思考的准确率与token消耗，缺乏对过度思考严重程度的参照，也缺乏对不同终止策略理论缓解能力的统一衡量标准。

Method: 1) 提出STP：对每条固定采样的推理轨迹，沿多个保留前缀重复生成答案；以每条轨迹中最早达到最大经验准确率的位置为参照，定义Overthinking Ratio（后续生成的思考token占比）与Accuracy Degradation（从参照到完整思考的准确率下降）。2) 将动态路由、固定预算、动态早停统一建模，量化各终止策略的理论缓解潜力。3) 基于该统一框架，用完整STP测量在归一化思考token预算下为每种策略构建token-准确率Pareto前沿。4) 构建可复用基准OTBench。

Result: OTBench基于STP流程生成，包含705亿（70.5B）推理token及对应探测测量，覆盖六个模型与四个基准组。

Conclusion: STP为过度思考提供了模型-任务层面的量化参照指标，统一框架实现了终止策略理论缓解潜力的可比评估，OTBench则为后续过度思考研究提供可复用的大规模基准。

Abstract: Reasoning models can benefit from test-time scaling, but continued reasoning may become ineffective or harmful, a phenomenon termed overthinking. Evaluations against full thinking compare accuracy and token usage but lack references for overthinking severity and strategies' theoretical mitigation potential. To characterize overthinking at the model-task level, we first introduce Step-wise Truncation Probing (STP), repeatedly generating answers from multiple retained prefixes along each fixed sampled reasoning trajectory. Using each trajectory's earliest measured position attaining maximum empirical accuracy as a reference, we define Overthinking Ratio and Accuracy Degradation as the fraction of thinking tokens generated afterward and the accuracy decrease from this reference to full thinking, respectively. Secondly, we then formulate dynamic routing, fixed budgets, and dynamic early exit within a unified framework to quantify the theoretical mitigation potential of different termination strategies. Based on this unified framework, we use the complete STP measurements to construct a token-accuracy Pareto frontier for each strategy under a common normalized thinking-token budget. We further develop OTBench, a reusable benchmark built from the STP process, which produced 70.5B reasoning tokens and the corresponding probing measurements across six models and four benchmark groups.

</details>


### [146] [MENTOR: Learning from Failure with On-Policy Pedagogical Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13594)
*Guanlan Yue,Hang Yu,Xiaoyong Huang,Ruipeng Li,Wei Zhang,Bingchang Liu,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MENTOR，一个面向 RLVR 中“零奖励崩溃”问题的多智能体在线教学干预框架。它实时诊断失败根因并动态生成针对性指令提示，作为即插即用模块接入 GRPO 等策略优化流程。在数学与代码生成任务上有效缓解零奖励崩溃，超越现有最优基线，在 Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct 上实现最高 78.3% 的平均相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 是大推理模型的关键训练范式，但存在“零奖励崩溃”：任务难度骤增导致学习信号消失。传统做法直接丢弃失败轨迹，浪费了其中关于模型认知缺口的诊断信息。现有解决方案多退回静态的“离策略式”干预（如预定义提示库）或事后修正，缺乏自适应、前瞻性的引导，难以帮助模型摆脱崩溃。

Method: 提出向“在策略教学干预”的范式转变，并设计 MENTOR——一个充当实时导师的多智能体框架。它实时诊断失败的根本原因，动态生成有针对性的教学提示，引导学习智能体进行下一次尝试。作为即插即用模块，MENTOR 可无缝集成到 GRPO 等现有策略优化流程中，无需修改训练基础设施。

Result: 在数学与代码生成任务上的大量实验表明，MENTOR 有效缓解了零奖励崩溃，并优于当前最先进的基线方法。值得注意的是，在 Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct 上相较其基座模型取得了最高达 78.3% 的平均相对增益。

Conclusion: MENTOR 通过将失败轨迹转化为诊断信号并进行在策略、实时、自适应的教学干预，为 RLVR 中的零奖励崩溃问题提供了有效且易于集成的解决思路，并在数学与代码任务上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is a key driver for Large Reasoning Models, but it is vulnerable to "zero-reward collapse", a training failure where a sudden increase in task difficulty causes the learning signal to vanish. We argue that the conventional approach of discarding these failed trajectories is wasteful, as they contain valuable diagnostic information about the model's cognitive gaps, a bright side that can be leveraged for recovery. Existing solutions, however, often regress to static, "off-policy-style" interventions like pre-defined hint libraries or perform post-hoc refinement, failing to provide the adaptive, forward-looking guidance needed to escape the collapse. To address this, we propose a paradigm shift towards on-policy pedagogical intervention and introduce MENTOR, a multi-agent framework that acts as a live mentor. MENTOR diagnoses the root cause of failure in real-time and dynamically generates targeted, instructional prompts to guide the learning agent's next attempt. As a plug-and-play module, MENTOR integrates seamlessly into existing policy optimization pipelines like GRPO without modifying the training infrastructure. Extensive experiments on math and code generation show that MENTOR effectively mitigates zero-reward collapse, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. Notably, it achieves massive average relative gains of up to 78.3% on Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct over its base model.

</details>


### [147] [Humanize Olympiad Agents: A Multi-Session Workflow for Olympiad Problem Solving with Solve, Review, Wiki, Verify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13632)
*Zhengyang Zhang,Jing Xiong,Hongzhou Lin,Juihui Chung,Zijian Zhang,Song Han,Sihao Liu,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HOA，一个开源的流程级多智能体协作框架，通过独立评审、结构化知识库和 Lean 证明验证器三条反馈通道提升长时程科学与奥赛任务表现，在 IMO/IPhO/IOI 2026 取得满分，并在开放模型上接近甚至超越闭源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体在长时程科学与奥赛任务上进展迅速，但存在两个问题：一是相关系统未同时公开底层模型与完整推理栈；二是单智能体会话在难题上脆弱，早期错误会传播、领域知识缺失会阻碍进展、表面成功可能掩盖对原任务的误解或弱化。

Method: 提出 HOA（Humanize Olympiads Agent），一个开源、流程级（flow-level）框架，协调多个智能体会话，通过三条互补反馈通道：独立评审者对候选解答进行评估并给出可操作反馈；结构化 wiki 提供任务特定知识，弥补或纠正预训练中缺失/不可靠的内容；验证器将生成的 Lean 证明与可信题目陈述进行核对。

Result: 使用 GPT-5.6 Sol 时，HOA 在 IMO、IPhO、IOI 2026 取得满分，IChO 2026 接近满分，并解出 PutnamBench 全部 672 题（100%）；在 Lean-Eval 排行榜以 251/304（82.6%）位列第一。使用开放模型 Kimi-K3 时，HOA 在 IMO、IPhO、IOI 取得满分，IChO 达金牌级 97.1%。

Conclusion: 在 IMO 和 IPhO 上，HOA+Kimi-K3 与闭源 GPT-5.6 Sol + /Goal 基线接近，在 IChO 和 IOI 上则超过该基线，表明系统性反馈机制可帮助弥合基础模型能力上的显著差距。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are rapidly improving on long-horizon scientific and olympiad tasks. Recent systems have produced a Lean-formalized resolution of the Navier--Stokes problem and autonomous solutions to four open questions in the Erdős Conjectures database. Yet neither system releases both its underlying model and complete inference stack. More broadly, a single agent session remains brittle on difficult problems: early errors can propagate, missing domain knowledge can stall progress, and apparent success can mask a misinterpretation or weakening of the original task. We introduce HOA (Humanize Olympiads Agent), an open, flow-level framework that coordinates multiple agent sessions through three complementary feedback channels. First, an independent reviewer evaluates each candidate and provides actionable feedback. Second, a structured wiki supplies task-specific knowledge that may be missing or unreliable in model pretraining. Third, a verifier checks generated Lean proofs against trusted challenge statements. With GPT-5.6 Sol, HOA achieves perfect scores on the IMO, IPhO, and IOI 2026, near-full marks on the IChO 2026 problem set, and solves all 672 problems (100%) on PutnamBench. On the Lean-Eval leaderboard, HOA ranks first, solving 251 of 304 problems (82.6%). We further evaluate HOA with open models. Using Kimi-K3, HOA achieves full scores on the IMO, IPhO, and IOI, and a gold-level score of 97.1% on the IChO. These results closely match the closed-source GPT-5.6 Sol + /Goal baseline on the IMO and IPhO, while exceeding it on the IChO and IOI, demonstrating that systematic feedback can help bridge substantial differences in base-model capability.

</details>


### [148] [Learning Size Independent Constraint Models from Input Data and Positive and Negative Example Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13640)
*Helmut Simonis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种约束获取工具，能从多实例的输入数据及正负样例解中生成与规模无关的约束程序，并在PTHG21挑战赛例子上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束建模通常依赖人工完成且模型与问题规模绑定，难以复用到未见数据；本文旨在自动从样例中获取可泛化的规模无关模型，降低建模成本并提升复用性。

Method: 工具先在全局约束与二元约束的典型约束模式上，为每个问题实例发现规模相关的特定模型，再将多个实例的模型泛化为一个规模无关模型，最终输出MiniZinc代码及模型的文本描述。

Result: 在PTHG21挑战赛的示例问题上进行了评估，结果表明该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 基于典型约束模式的约束获取可实现规模无关模型的自动生成与代码输出，为约束程序自动建模提供了一条可行途径。

Abstract: In this paper we describe a Constraint Acquisition Tool that generates size independent constraint programs from input data and positive and negative example solutions of multiple problem instances. The system finds size specific models based on typical constraint patterns over global and binary constraints, then generalizes the models to a size independent model that can be run on unseen input data. The system produces MiniZinc code as well as a textual description of the model. We evaluate the tool on the example problems of the PTHG21 Challenge and show the feasibility of the approach.

</details>


### [149] [A Step Harder, Not a Step Too Far: The Benefits and Limits of Curriculum Difficulty Progression in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13669)
*Ronghan Li,Fei Chen,Mingyu Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究GRPO中课程学习难度选择策略：在演变策略下重新估计样本难度，并设计分阶段课程逐步将难度带向更难样本迁移。GSM8K实验表明，持续选择中等难度样本并非最优，适度向更难难度带推进可将测试准确率提升至0.6591±0.0077，但过度推进反而下降至0.6405±0.0053，说明收益呈非单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GRPO在线采样中，中等难度样本更易产生有信息量的组相对学习信号，因此常被优先选择以提升训练效率。但持续选择这类样本是否能带来最强的最终模型性能尚不明确，训练是否应逐步向更难样本推进仍待验证。同时，样本难度随策略演化而变化，固定的难度估计会与当前策略失配。

Method: 在当前策略下重新估计样本难度；借鉴课程学习由易到难的原则，设计并评估一种分阶段课程，使活跃难度带逐步向更难样本迁移；在GSM8K上进行实验，比较固定中等难度课程与不同推进程度的分阶段课程。 

Result: 持续选择中等难度样本并未取得观测到的最强性能。在三个随机种子下，推进至中等偏难难度带时测试准确率为0.6591±0.0077，优于固定中等难度课程的0.6449±0.0024；但进一步推进至更难难度带时准确率降至0.6405±0.0053。

Conclusion: 分阶段难度推进可以优于持续的中等难度选择，但其收益是非单调的。有效的GRPO课程应受益于超越中等难度的推进，同时避免过度向过难样本迁移。

Abstract: Curriculum learning typically organizes training samples from easy to hard. In GRPO, medium-difficulty samples are attractive for online selection because they are more likely to yield informative group-relative learning signals, and prioritizing them can improve training efficiency. However, it remains unclear whether persistently selecting such samples also leads to the strongest final model performance, or whether training can benefit from progressing toward harder samples. Meanwhile, sample difficulty changes as the policy evolves, so fixed difficulty estimates can become misaligned with the current policy. We therefore re-estimate sample difficulty under the current policy and, inspired by the easy-to-hard principle of curriculum learning, design and evaluate a staged curriculum that progressively shifts the active difficulty band toward harder samples. Experiments on GSM8K show that persistent medium-difficulty selection does not achieve the strongest observed performance. Across three random seeds, progressing to a moderately harder difficulty band improves test accuracy to $0.6591 \pm 0.0077$, compared with $0.6449 \pm 0.0024$ for the fixed-medium curriculum. However, further progression to a harder difficulty band reduces accuracy to $0.6405 \pm 0.0053$. These results show that staged difficulty progression can outperform persistent medium-difficulty selection, but its benefit is non-monotonic. Effective GRPO curricula can therefore benefit from progressing beyond medium difficulty, while avoiding excessive progression toward overly difficult samples.}

</details>


### [150] [A Baseline Assessment for the Agentic Web Technology Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13703)
*Vagner Figueredo de Santana,Diogo Cortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MASTIF（多智能体系统测试框架），用于评估Agentic Web技术，并对3个LLM、3种协议和4个智能体实现框架在300个Mind2Web基准任务上进行基线评测，表明按任务选择技术栈可提升任务完成率、效率和社会价值稳定性，同时降低冗余度和完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的协议、框架和技术快速涌现，导致复杂局面：开发者在构建最先进AI方案时缺乏对各类协议、框架和库如何协同组成Agentic Web方案的评估，即便在加入生态之前就面临技能、治理、基础设施等挑战。因此需要一个系统化评估方法并提供基线参考。

Method: 提出名为MASTIF（Multi-Agent System TestIng Framework）的Agentic Web评估系统；对3个LLM、3种协议、4个智能体实现框架进行基线评估，评测任务为300个Mind2Web基准任务。

Result: 选择任务特定的技术栈可带来任务完成率、效率得分和社会价值稳定性得分的提升，并降低冗余度（verbosity）和任务完成时间。

Conclusion: 该工作为该复杂且快速演进的场景提供了指标与结果的基线，供设计、评估和开发多智能体系统的研究者与开发者参考。

Abstract: The fast pace in which protocols, frameworks, and technologies are being proposed for LLM-based agents results in a complex scenario for anyone aiming to create state-of-the-art AI solutions. Without an assessment on how different protocols, frameworks, and libraries work together to compose an Agentic Web solution, developers and organizations face different challenges (e.g., skilling, governance, infrastructure) even before becoming part of the Agentic Web ecosystem. In this context, we propose a system for performing Agentic Web evaluations called MASTIF (Multi-Agent System TestIng Framework) and present a baseline assessment of Agentic Web technologies, including 3 LLMs, 3 protocols, and 4 agent implementation frameworks, against 300 Mind2Web benchmark tasks. Results suggest that selecting a task-specific technology stack may result in gains of task completion rate, efficiency score, and social value stability scores; and reductions in verbosity and time to complete tasks. With the presented results, we expect to present a baseline in terms of metrics and results for this complex and fast evolving scenario for those aiming at designing, assessing, and developing multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [151] [Adaptive DHiSparse: Speculative Decoding over a Load-Adaptive KV Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13708)
*Naji Alazhar,Wassel Alazhar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Adaptive DHiSparse，在分层稀疏 KV 上支持投机解码并随负载自适应调整 KV 驻留，显著提升长上下文服务吞吐与并发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文服务受限于 KV 显存：在硬件达到有效吞吐前 KV 显存已耗尽。HiSparse 将历史存于主机内存并按需取回，但仅用单一缓存预算且不支持 DSpark、DFlash2 等草稿模型投机解码，导致显存利用与负载自适应不足。

Method: 在分层稀疏 KV 上实现投机解码并随负载调整驻留：保护必需工作集，显存空闲时保留更多历史，其余通过精确融合检索恢复；DSpark/DFlash2 保持原提议与接受规则，查询共享缺失行检索，草稿 KV 使用窗口受限环形缓冲，验证暂存跨层组复用；可选驻留在负载变化时释放内存。

Result: 100K 上下文、并发 128 时，GLM-5.3 配 DFlash2 每解码 GPU 达 793 输出 token/s，为 HiSparse 的 1.45 倍、非投机自适应服务的 1.27 倍；相同延迟界下并发提升至 4 倍，输出为 2.3-3.7 倍。DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 在 500K 上下文、并发 512 时配 DSpark 输出为常驻服务的 1.85 倍；负载步进中主机取回占比由 14.2% 降至 1.4%，高负载下释放显存。

Conclusion: Adaptive DHiSparse 通过分层稀疏 KV 上的投机解码与负载自适应驻留，在不改变草稿模型规则的前提下显著提升长上下文服务的吞吐、并发与显存效率。

Abstract: Long-context serving runs out of KV memory before the hardware reaches its useful throughput. HiSparse stores history in host memory and fetches only the selected entries, but it uses one cache budget at every load and does not handle draft-model speculative decoding such as DSpark and DFlash2. We present Adaptive DHiSparse, which supports speculative decoding over hierarchical sparse KV and adapts residency to load. It protects the required working set, retains additional history while GPU memory is spare, and recovers the rest through exact fused retrieval. DSpark and DFlash2 keep their proposal and acceptance rules: queries share missing-row retrieval, draft KV uses a window-bounded ring, and verification staging is reused across layer groups. At 100K context and concurrency 128, GLM-5.3 with DFlash2 delivers 793 output tokens/s per decode GPU, 1.45$\times$ HiSparse and 1.27$\times$ non-speculative adaptive serving. Held to the same latency bounds as HiSparse, it sustains up to 4$\times$ the concurrency and 2.3 to 3.7$\times$ the output, depending on the per-token bound. On DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash at 500K context and concurrency 512, Adaptive DHiSparse with DSpark delivers 1.85$\times$ the output of resident serving with DSpark. In a load step, optional residency reduces host fetches from 14.2% to 1.4% of selected rows at low load and releases memory under pressure.

</details>


### [152] [A Privileged Teacher That Only Scores Cannot Search: Critique-Conditioned On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13744)
*Mayug Maniparambil,Ayush Rai,Ekansh Verma,Noel E. O'Connor,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出on-policy自蒸馏的评分信号（full-distribution KL与token log-ratio）既不定位错误也不纠正错误，原因是定位错误本身是搜索问题而评分教师未被告知执行该搜索；作者提出critique-conditioned self-distillation，先生成批评再据此条件化教师，从而获得可定位、可纠正并将概率推向回退性token的信号，并在测试时恢复与GRPO冷启动中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy自蒸馏被寄望于用稠密的token级credit替代可验证奖励RL中稀疏的结果信号：以参考解为条件的教师对学生的自身rollout打分，并将分布偏移蒸馏回策略。作者要检验这一打分信号是否真正含有纠正性credit，即它能否定位错误并抑制错误token的概率。

Method: 利用学生rollout中的gold error spans，对自蒸馏目标所消费的两种信号——full-distribution KL与token log-ratio——进行两项性质测试：（1）是否定位错误；（2）是否将概率推离错误的student token，并检验其能否可靠区分同一问题下正确与错误的rollout。在此基础上提出critique-conditioned self-distillation：首先生成对当前rollout的批评，再将评分教师条件化于该批评之上。

Result: full-distribution KL与token log-ratio既不能定位错误，也不能将概率推离错误token，且无法在同一问题内可靠区分正确与错误的rollout。作者认为该失败是结构性的：在推理轨迹中定位错误本身是一个搜索问题，而评分教师从未被明确要求执行该搜索；同一模型在相同privileged information下，若被允许生成批评，则错误定位能力显著提升。采用critique-conditioned self-distillation后，所得信号能够定位错误、在error spans内具有纠正性，并将概率推向“Wait”“But”“Actually”等触发回退的token。

Conclusion: 批评信号在测试时与训练时均有用：在测试时将批评置于上下文中恢复错误rollout，可解决模型靠采样无法解决的数学问题；在cold-start GRPO中，它为可验证奖励本身无法提供信号的prompt提供了advantage信号。这说明自蒸馏的有效credit来自显式生成的批评式搜索，而非评分教师隐含的分布偏移。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation promises to replace the sparse outcome signal of verifiable-reward RL with dense token-level credit: a teacher conditioned on the reference solution scores the student's own rollout, and the resulting distributional shift is distilled back into the policy. We investigate whether this scoring signal actually contains corrective credit. Using gold error spans in student rollouts, we test the two signals consumed by self-distillation objectives, the full-distribution KL and the token log-ratio, for two properties: whether they localize errors and whether they push probability away from erroneous student tokens. They exhibit neither of these properties nor can they reliably distinguish correct from incorrect rollouts within the same problem. We argue that this failure is structural: locating an error in a reasoning trace is itself a search problem, while a scoring teacher is never explicitly asked to perform that search. The same model, with the same privileged information, localizes errors substantially better when allowed to generate a critique. This forms the motivation for \emph{critique-conditioned self-distillation}: first generate a critique of the current rollout, then condition the scoring teacher on that critique. The resulting signal localizes errors, is corrective within error spans, and shifts probability toward tokens that initiate backtracking such as ``Wait'', ``But'', and ``Actually''. We further show that the critique is useful both at test time, where resuming a wrong rollout with the critique in context converts math problems the model cannot solve by sampling, and in cold-start GRPO, where it provides an advantage signal on prompts for which verifiable rewards alone provide none.

</details>


### [153] [UkrMeme: Multimodal Explainable Persuasion Detection in Ukrainian Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13783)
*Nazar Andrushko,Vera Schmitt,Veronika Solopova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究乌克兰模因中的说服技巧检测，构建UkrMeme数据集（200条乌克兰战时模因）及PTM-UA（Propaganda 950部分子集的乌克兰语翻译版本），在零样本提示下用视觉语言模型进行图像、文本和多模态输入的评估，采用层级F1指标，并比较LRP与模型生成token归因方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模因在现代在线沟通中作用关键，在政治冲突语境下视觉与文本线索结合传递说服性信息。现有研究缺乏针对乌克兰语模因的说服技巧检测基准与方法评估。

Method: 构建UkrMeme（200条乌克兰战时模因的说服技巧标注数据集）与PTM-UA（保留原始视觉、翻译文本的Propaganda 950子集乌克兰语版）；在零样本提示设置下评估视觉语言模型在图像、文本及多模态输入上的表现，使用层级F1指标；比较Layer-wise Relevance Propagation（LRP）与模型生成token归因，分析各解释方法强调证据的语义类别。

Result: 论文仅描述评估框架与数据集构建，摘要未提供具体数值或定量结果。

Conclusion: 研究为乌克兰语模因说服技巧检测提供新数据集与基准，建立了VLMs零样本多模态评估流程，并通过多种归因方法比较揭示模型解释所依赖的证据语义类别。

Abstract: Memes play a central role in modern online communication, especially in contexts of political conflict where visual and textual cues combine to convey persuasive messages. We study persuasive technique detection in Ukrainian memes using vision-language models (VLMs). We introduce UkrMeme, a dataset of 200 organically produced Ukrainian wartime memes annotated for persuasion techniques, and PTM-UA, a Ukrainian translation of a subset of the Propaganda 950 benchmark that preserves original visual content while translating meme text. We evaluate VLMs in a zero-shot prompting setup across image, text, and multimodal inputs using hierarchical F1 metrics. Finally, we compare Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) and model-generated token attributions and analyze the semantic categories of evidence emphasized by each explanation method.

</details>


### [154] [Studying Comics at Scale with Artificial Intelligence: The PAGE Framework and Three Studies of American Comics and Japanese Manga](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13840)
*Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出PAGE框架，将人工智能（含生成模型）用作漫画研究工具，对扫描页面进行分格、文本与书目解析，并用视觉语言模型按编码手册逐页标注，再以人工标注校验。框架在3,929本美国漫画（1938–1956）和87卷日本漫画上通过三项研究验证，分别用于还原政治权力形象、测量页面视觉形式并识别艺术家风格、追踪对话归属。作者还提出利用同一工具使档案可按图像内容检索、服务盲人与低视力读者、保护脆弱藏品并为无名艺术家署名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漫画是二十世纪重要的视觉记录，包含数十万页内容，记录了大众读者所见的领导人、战争与英雄。然而这一记录大多未被整体研究，漫画研究长期以单本书为单位进行。作者认为人工智能特别是生成模型可以改变这一状况，服务于漫画媒介本身。

Method: 提出PAGE框架，使AI成为漫画研究工具：1）将每个扫描件解析为分格、文本和书目记录；2）使用视觉语言模型按书面编码手册逐页标注，模拟人工编码者；3）将部分解读样本与人工标注对齐验证；4）解释整个档案库的变化，并对出版商和体裁进行控制。在美国漫画3,929本（1938–1956）和87卷日本漫画上演示，分三项研究，每项依赖不同能力。

Result: 第一项研究结合文本检索与视觉阅读，还原大众媒介如何描绘政治权力；第二项研究测量整个行业的页面视觉形式，并识别个别艺术家的手法；第三项研究追踪两种漫画传统中“谁在说话”，并与人工标注核对。三项研究均基于不同AI能力得到验证。

Conclusion: 除研究用途外，同一套工具还可让档案按“画了什么”进行检索，使盲人和低视力读者能够接触漫画，帮助保护脆弱藏品，并为未署名的艺术家确认署名。作者提出一项研究议程，主张人工智能可加强漫画媒介的研究、保存与传播。

Abstract: Comics are one of the great visual records of the twentieth century: hundreds of thousands of pages in which a mass readership saw its leaders, its wars and its heroes. Most of this record has never been studied as a whole; comics scholarship proceeds one book at a time. We argue that artificial intelligence, including generative models, can change this to the benefit of the medium itself. We propose PAGE, a framework that makes artificial intelligence an instrument for comics research. It parses every scan into panels, text and a bibliographic record; annotates each page with a vision-language model that follows a written codebook, as a human coder would; grounds samples of these readings in human annotation; and explains change across an archive, with estimates controlled for publisher and genre. We demonstrate the framework on 3,929 American comic books (1938--1956) and 87 volumes of Japanese manga, in three studies, each resting on a different capability. The first pairs text search with visual reading to recover how a mass medium portrayed political power. The second measures the page's visual form across an industry and recovers the hand of individual artists. The third follows who speaks across two comics traditions, checked against human annotation. Beyond research, the same instruments can make archives searchable by what is drawn, open comics to blind and low-vision readers, and help preserve fragile collections and credit uncredited artists. We outline a research agenda in which artificial intelligence strengthens the study, preservation and reach of the medium.

</details>


### [155] [X-AgentRed: Red-Teaming Personal Agents in Evolving Multi-Service Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13886)
*Yeonsung Jung,Zhiyuan Wang,Zifan Wang,Vítor Albiero,Benjamin Newman,Edoardo Debenedetti,Ivan Evtimov,Ninareh Mehrabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自演化红队框架X-AgentRed，基于工具依赖图发现并扩展有害工具调用路径，有害路径率达STAC的1.77倍，工具覆盖率达2.99倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人智能体通过工具跨服务行动，其效果可累积与交互，在满足良性请求时也可能产生非预期或有害结果。要主动识别这类组合性失效，需系统探索多样的跨服务路径。现有红队方法多依赖提示LLM从任务与环境描述中寻找有害路径，或将有害目标分解为看似良性的动作组合，生成结果偏向训练中习得的模式，倾向熟悉的工具组合，导致新颖的跨服务交互探索不足。

Method: X-AgentRed是一种自演化红队框架。它分析每个工具的效果、潜在危害与执行前置条件，构建有向图以刻画“某工具的效果如何满足另一工具的执行前置条件”。从图中采样配方（recipes），实例化为可令智能体产生有害结果的真实场景。利用执行反馈自我改进：通过工具替换多样化产生危害的路径，并为失败路径中被阻断的步骤精炼前置条件以促成进一步执行。

Result: 在四个前沿智能体模型上，X-AgentRed的有害路径率达STAC的1.77倍，并探索了更多样化的工具集，平均工具覆盖率达STAC的2.99倍。

Conclusion: 以工具依赖图为结构先验并结合执行反馈的自演化机制，可有效缓解生成偏向熟悉工具组合的问题，实现更高效、更多样的跨服务有害路径发现，为智能体组合性风险的红队评估提供新范式。

Abstract: Personal agents increasingly connect to services across the web and applications by using tools. As the effects of their actions accumulate and interact across services, unintended or harmful outcomes can arise when agents fulfill benign user requests. Proactively identifying these compositional failures requires systematically exploring diverse cross-service paths. Existing red-teaming methods often simply prompt LLMs to identify potentially harmful paths from task and environment descriptions or decompose harmful objectives specified by humans or LLMs into seemingly benign action combinations. However, generation is biased toward patterns learned during training, potentially favoring familiar tool combinations and leaving novel cross-service interactions underexplored. To address this, we propose X-AgentRed, a self-evolving red-teaming framework that discovers harmful tool call paths through a graph of tool dependencies and expands and refines them using execution feedback. By analyzing each tool's effects, potential harms, and preconditions for execution, the red-teaming agent constructs a directed graph that captures how one tool's effects can satisfy another's execution preconditions. Recipes sampled from this graph are instantiated as realistic scenarios in which agents can produce harmful outcomes. Execution feedback enables self-improvement by diversifying paths that produce harm through tool substitution and refining preconditions at blocked steps in unsuccessful paths to enable further execution. Across four frontier agentic models, X-AgentRed achieves $1.77\times$ the harmful path rate of STAC and explores a more diverse set of tools, yielding $2.99\times$ the tool coverage on average.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Beyond the Final Embedding: Retrieving Memories from Token-Level Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13277)
*Han Luo,Zhan Zhuang,Xu He,Jujia Zhao,Yu Li,Suqin Yuan,Dong Li,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的稀疏token匹配检索方法，利用编码器已有的上下文token表示进行细粒度局部证据匹配，并可选低秩查询适配器，在八个基准上提升检索与问答表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于嵌入的检索器多采用查询与记忆的单向量表示进行比对，但当相关性仅依赖内容的一小部分时，单向量表示难以捕捉此类局部证据，导致检索效果受限。

Method: 利用同一嵌入模型已计算的上下文token表示，进行稀疏token匹配：每个查询token在候选记忆中检索少量匹配token，将匹配分数按记忆聚合，并与整条记忆相似度结合以排序。可选地训练一个低秩查询适配器，在固定编码器与存储记忆表示的前提下引入监督信号。

Result: 在八个基准和多个模型族上无需额外训练即提升检索效果；引入查询适配后在Qwen3-Embedding-4B上平均Recall@10与NDCG@10分别再提升13.3和14.3个百分点。检索增益进一步转化为长期记忆基准上更高的问答准确率。

Conclusion: 稀疏token匹配可有效补充单向量相似度，实现免训练检索增强，且查询适配器能进一步放大收益，并下游提升长期记忆问答性能。

Abstract: Memory-augmented assistants retrieve evidence from stored conversations and documents to answer queries. Most embedding-based retrievers compare single-vector representations of queries and memories, even when relevance depends on a small part of the content. Such local evidence can be matched to queries using the contextual token representations already computed by the same embedding models. Our method uses sparse token matching to improve retrieval without additional training. Each query token retrieves a small set of matching tokens across candidate memories. The matching scores are aggregated for each memory and combined with whole-memory similarity to rank memories. An optional low-rank query adapter can be trained with supervision while keeping the encoder and stored memory representations fixed. Our method improves retrieval across eight benchmarks and multiple model families without additional training. Query adaptation further increases the gains to 13.3 and 14.3 percentage points in average Recall@10 and NDCG@10 on Qwen3-Embedding-4B. These retrieval gains translate into higher question-answering accuracy on long-term memory benchmarks.

</details>


### [157] [PRIME: Pre-Retrieval Index Modeling Enhancement for Structured Document Retrieval-Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13296)
*Cheuk Hei Chung,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出索引侧优化框架PRIME，通过语义重构与元数据合成两条路径提升检索质量，显著降低索引成本并提升答案F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息检索研究主要优化查询侧检索器（如ColBERT、BGE）和生成器（如Self-RAG、GraphRAG），而将语料侧表示简化为分块处理，导致索引侧质量这一优化杠杆未被充分探索。

Method: PRIME框架包含两条互补路径：（1）LLM驱动的语义重构（SR），将长文档转化为结构化、信息密集的章节；（2）确定性的元数据合成（MS）与层级检索（HR），提取语义锚点和标题结构以实现由粗到细的章节检索。在QASPER、BRIGHT和NFCorpus上评估，并考察检索器与重排器的影响。

Result: 确定性元数据达到结构感知分块的检索质量，同时将嵌入计算减少45.8倍、索引向量数量减少22.4倍；完整部署中语义重构进一步将Answer-F1提升13.7%，并刻画了质量-保真度-成本的权衡。在三个数据集上归纳出三种定性机制，说明索引侧优化何时有益或有害，且这些效果在强重排器下被中和。

Conclusion: 索引侧优化是提升LLM智能体检索的有效且此前被忽视的方向；元数据合成可大幅降低索引成本而不损失检索质量，语义重构能进一步提升答案质量，但其收益取决于具体机制并受强重排器影响而被削弱。

Abstract: High-precision retrieval over external knowledge bases is essential for LLM agents to operate effectively. However, existing Information Retrieval (IR) research primarily optimizes query-side retrievers (e.g., ColBERT, BGE) and generators (e.g., Self-RAG, GraphRAG), while reducing corpus-side representation to chunking. This leaves index-side quality an underexplored lever. We propose Pre-Retrieval Index Modeling & Enhancement (PRIME), an index-side optimization framework built on two complementary tracks: an LLM-driven Semantic Restructuring (SR) track that transforms long documents into structured, information-dense sections, and a deterministic Metadata Synthesis (MS) and Hierarchical Retrieval (HR) track that extracts semantic anchors and heading structure for coarse-to-fine section retrieval. Our evaluations show that deterministic metadata matches structure-aware chunking's retrieval quality while cutting embedding computation by 45.8 $\times$ and index vector count by 22.4 $\times$. On top of these gains, Semantic Restructuring (SR) further improves Answer-F1 by 13.7% in full deployment, with characterized quality-fidelity-cost trade-offs. Across QASPER, BRIGHT, and NFCorpus, we further characterize three qualitative regimes that determine whether index-side optimization helps or degrades retrieval, while showing that these index-side effects are neutralized under a strong reranker.

</details>


### [158] [NEAR: Neighborhood-Aware Residuals for Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13314)
*Han Luo,Zhan Zhuang,Xu He,Jujia Zhao,Yu Li,Suqin Yuan,Dong Li,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练的NEighborhood-Aware Residuals（NEAR）框架，利用记忆记录间共享信息构造局部参考，生成补充原始嵌入的残差表示，以提升记忆检索的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存储记录之间可能共享主题、实体或事件，这种共享内容会使缺乏所需证据的记录获得较高相似度分数，从而排在正确证据之前，干扰查询-记忆匹配。

Method: NEAR为每条记忆从其邻居中推导参考方向，查询则从预计算表中获取参考；基于这些参考，为查询和记忆构造投影残差，并将残差与原始嵌入结合，在固定记忆索引上进行一次近似最近邻搜索。该框架无需训练。

Result: 在八个长上下文检索基准上，NEAR在九个嵌入模型上均取得一致提升；下游评估显示其改善了问答性能。

Conclusion: 利用记录间共享信息构造的邻域感知残差可作为原始嵌入的有效补充，在不训练的前提下显著提升长上下文记忆检索与下游问答表现。

Abstract: Memory retrieval aims to identify evidence from stored records to answer a query. Stored records with different factual details may share topics, entities, or events. Such shared contents would make records without the required evidence more likely to receive high similarity scores and outrank the correct evidence. In this paper, we use the information shared by these records to construct local references for query-memory matching. We propose NEighborhood-Aware Residuals (NEAR), a training-free framework that uses these references to construct residual representations that complement the original embeddings. Specifically, each memory derives a reference direction from its neighbors, and each query obtains its reference from a precomputed table. Using these references, NEAR constructs projection residuals for both queries and memories. These residuals are combined with the original embeddings for a single approximate nearest-neighbor search over a fixed memory index. Experiments on eight long-context retrieval benchmarks show consistent improvements across nine embedding models. Downstream evaluations show that NEAR improves question-answering performance.

</details>


### [159] [SPARQL-LLM in the TEXT2SPARQL 2026 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13648)
*Panayiotis Smeros,Vincent Emonet,Ruijie Wang,Ana-Claudia Sima,Tarcisio Mendes de Farias*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SPARQL-LLM是一个开源的知识图谱问答系统，在TEXT2SPARQL 2026挑战赛中综合排名第一，准确率最高比第二名高59%，支持英德西三语问答，已公开发布并部署于生物信息学知识图谱生产环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过SPARQL查询分布式知识图谱仍十分困难，用户需掌握查询语法、端点规范与模式细节。知识图谱问答（KGQA）系统旨在自动将自然语言翻译为SPARQL，而大语言模型（LLM）的最新进展使这一翻译愈发可行。

Method: 提出开源KGQA系统SPARQL-LLM，在TEXT2SPARQL 2026挑战赛背景下，利用LLM将自然语言问题自动翻译为SPARQL查询，以检索分布式知识图谱数据，并支持多语言输入。

Result: 在TEXT2SPARQL 2026挑战赛中，SPARQL-LLM在全部数据集上于八种参赛方法中综合排名第一，准确率最高较第二名高出59%，并支持英语、德语和西班牙语的多语言问答。

Conclusion: SPARQL-LLM已开源于GitHub（https://github.com/sib-swiss/sparql-llm），并部署于去中心化生物信息学知识图谱的生产环境（https://www.expasy.org/chat），验证了其实际可用性与领先性能。

Abstract: Querying distributed data represented as Knowledge Graphs (KGs) via SPARQL remains a difficult task, requiring fluency in query syntax, endpoint specifications, and schema details. Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA) systems address this by automatically translating natural language into SPARQL, and recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have made this translation increasingly feasible. In this paper, we describe SPARQL-LLM, our open-source KGQA system, in the context of the TEXT2SPARQL 2026 challenge. In this challenge, SPARQL-LLM ranks first overall among eight competing approaches across all datasets, achieving up to 59% higher accuracy than the second-best approach and supporting multilingual question answering in English, German, and Spanish. SPARQL-LLM is publicly released at https://github.com/sib-swiss/sparql-llm and deployed in production over decentralized bioinformatics KGs at https://www.expasy.org/chat.

</details>


### [160] [Tracing and Taming Hallucinated Terms via Subsequence-Association Filtering and Breadth-Aware Selection for LLM Query Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13810)
*Amit Kumar Jaiswal,Sagar Uprety,Prayag Tiwari*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出免训练、检索器无关的SAFE-QE查询扩展方法，基于"子序列联想"幻觉机制，对扩展词按语料条件跨上下文可复现性打分，将选择建模为伪相关集上的约束覆盖最大化，并新提出幻觉扩展词率(HETR)指标；在TREC/BEIR上nDCG@10与生成式扩展统计无差异，注入不支持词数大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为查询扩展词来源时会幻觉，注入不存在的实体和离题词，增加排序噪声、损害强检索器。现有补救措施（语料接地、相互验证、偏好对齐）把幻觉词当作无差别的噪声，靠一致性启发式过滤，既缺乏对某词为何出现的机制性解释，也缺乏"保留多少个词"的原则性判据。

Method: SAFE-QE（免训练、检索器无关），依据子序列联想幻觉解释：按"语料条件下的跨上下文可复现性"给每个候选扩展词打分——在随机化查询上下文中反复出现却得不到伪相关集支持者，被判为主导性幻觉联想的产物并降权。进而借鉴"一致性（无幻觉）与广度（覆盖）权衡"的语言生成刻画，将词选择表述为伪相关集上的约束覆盖最大化，保留近似广但一致、且重采样下稳定的词集。同时提出幻觉扩展词率(HETR)——查询侧忠实度指标，统计无任何相关文档支持的注入扩展词数。

Result: 在基准TREC和BEIR集合上，SAFE-QE的nDCG@10与生成式、语料引导式扩展统计无显著差异，但注入的不支持词更少：每查询4.3个，Query2doc为27.8个，HyDE为57.5个；在强学习稀疏检索器上（所有扩展方法均退化）同样保持该优势。忠实度在排序质量无可测代价的情况下即可获得。

Conclusion: 幻觉并非只能无差别过滤的噪声，可通过机制性判据加以识别与降权；以忠实度（少注入不支持词）为目标的查询扩展可在不牺牲nDCG@10的前提下实现，"忠实度零排序代价"。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now a standard source of query expansion terms but they hallucinate. They inject non existent entities and off-topic terms that add ranking noise and can hurt strong retrievers. Existing remedies such as corpus grounding, mutual verification and preference alignment treat hallucinated terms as an undifferentiated nuisance to be filtered by agreement heuristics, without a mechanistic account of why a given term appears or a principled criterion for how many terms to keep. We propose Subsequence-Association Filtered, breadth-aware query expansion (SAFE-QE), a training-free, retriever agnostic method that draws on the subsequence-association account of hallucination. We score each candidate expansion term by its corpus conditioned cross-context reproducibility, terms that reappear under randomised query contexts yet lack support in the pseudo-relevant set are flagged as products of dominant hallucinatory associations and down-weighted. Drawing on the characterisation of language generation as a trade-off between consistency (no hallucination) and breadth (coverage), we cast term selection as constrained coverage maximisation over the pseudo-relevant set, keeping a set that is approximately broad but consistent and stable across resampling. We also introduce the Hallucinated Expansion Term Rate, a query side faithfulness metric that counts injected expansion terms no relevant document supports. We evaluate our approach on benchmarked TREC and BEIR collections. Our SAFE-QE is statistically indistinguishable from generative and corpus-steered expansion on nDCG@10 while injecting fewer unsupported terms 4.3 per query against 27.8 for Query2doc and 57.5 for HyDE and on a strong learned sparse retriever, where every expansion method regresses. Faithfulness is therefore available at no measurable cost in ranking quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [SoftwareGym: Scaling Computer-Use Agent Training with Diverse Synthesized Software Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12611)
*Tian Jin,Wenhao Wang,Jing Kang,Haoting Shi,Rui Ye,Yijun Lu,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出自动化流水线 SoftwareGym，合成多样化 GUI 软件环境用于 CUA 训练，在 OSWorld 上 Pass@3 从 41.39% 提升至 56.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUA 快速发展，但大规模合成多样且可靠的软件环境仍具挑战：原生应用环境构建与集成成本高，导致应用覆盖面受限并易产生应用特定过拟合；浏览器承载环境则交互深度与工作流复杂度有限。

Method: SoftwareGym 流水线：生成结构多样的界面设计，将其实现为可运行应用，并通过 GUI 评估配合迭代修复保证功能可靠性；基于此合成 182 个应用覆盖 12 个类别，并构建含 120 个任务的 SoftwareGym-Bench，采集轨迹用于 CUA 训练。

Result: 合成应用的平均 GUI 任务通过率达 91.5%；训练后模型在严格域外的 OSWorld 基准上 Pass@3 由 41.39% 提升至 56.97%，在 SoftwareGym-Bench 上达到 40.0%。

Conclusion: 合成软件环境可作为可扩展且可迁移的监督来源，用于 CUA 训练，并展现出跨域泛化潜力。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) are rapidly improving, yet synthesizing diverse and reliable software environments at scale remains challenging. Native application environments require substantial construction and integration effort, which limits application coverage and increases the risk of application-specific overfitting. Browser-hosted environments provide limited interaction depth and workflow complexity. These limitations motivate SoftwareGym, an automated pipeline for synthesizing GUI software environments with diverse interfaces, executable functionality, and runtime-verified behavior. It generates structurally diverse designs, realizes them as runnable applications, and uses GUI evaluation with iterative repair to ensure functional reliability. Using SoftwareGym, we synthesize 182 applications across twelve categories and achieve a mean GUI task pass rate of 91.5%. We construct SoftwareGym-Bench, a 120-task evaluation suite spanning the same twelve categories, and use trajectories collected from these environments for CUA training. The resulting model improves Pass@3 from 41.39% to 56.97% on the strictly out-of-domain OSWorld benchmark and achieves 40.0% on SoftwareGym-Bench. These results demonstrate the potential of synthesized software environments as scalable and transferable supervision for CUA training.

</details>


### [162] [How Do Hardware Faults Manifest Differently in LLM Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12756)
*Aiden Rubey,Zachary Coalson,Cong Chen,Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Jonathan Petit,Sanghyun Hong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究大语言模型（LLM）智能体对硬件随机故障的鲁棒性，通过对3个基准、6814次执行（2400+无故障、4300+双比特权重故障注入）的评测，揭示智能体执行会把模型级损坏转化为系统级结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于LLM硬件故障鲁棒性的研究聚焦于模型级结果（如输出错误或生成失败），但LLM智能体会反复与工具和环境交互形成循环，可能改变硬件故障的表现形式，因此需要系统研究智能体层面的故障传播与影响。

Method: 在三个智能体基准上进行大规模评测，共6814次智能体执行，包括2400+次无故障运行与4300+次注入独立采样的随机双比特权重故障的运行，并追踪故障在智能体执行过程中的传播路径。

Result: 与以往研究不同，发现智能体执行将模型级损坏转化为四类系统级结果：(1) 智能体可从故障输出中恢复并仍正确完成任务；(2) 这种恢复增加计算与执行成本；(3) 少量故障引发细微但语义上有意义的错误，而智能体其余行为正常；(4) 对某些模型和场景，故障会改变安全与对齐行为。

Conclusion: 智能体执行显著改变了硬件故障的表现形式与后果，模型级故障评估不足以刻画智能体系统的真实鲁棒性，需从系统级视角评估智能体在硬件故障下的恢复能力、成本开销、隐性语义错误及安全对齐风险。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of the resilience of large language model (LLM) agents to random hardware-induced faults. Prior work on hardware fault resilience in LLMs focuses on model-level outcomes, such as incorrect outputs or generation failures. Yet LLM agents repeatedly interact with tools and environments, creating loops that can change how hardware faults manifest. We conduct an extensive evaluation across three agent benchmarks, comprising 6,814 agent executions: 2,400+ clean runs and 4,300+ fault-injected runs with independently sampled random two-bit weight faults. We trace how these faults propagate through agent execution. Unlike prior studies, we find that agentic execution transforms model-level corruption into distinct system-level outcomes: (1) agents can recover from faulty model outputs and still complete tasks correctly; (2) this recovery increases computational and execution costs; (3) a few faults cause subtle but semantically meaningful errors while the agent otherwise behaves normally; and (4) for some models and scenarios, faults can alter security and alignment behavior.

</details>


### [163] [SwingGuard: Risk-Conditioned Multi-Agent Swing Trading with Dual-Timescale Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12988)
*Junzhou Su,Yichen Luo,Yebo Feng,Teng Li,Jiahua Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出 SwingGuard，一个双时间尺度、仅做多的多智能体框架，用于风险条件化的加密货币波段策略生成：策略尺度生成目标仓位，治理尺度（5分钟）持续约束执行，在 BTC 与 ETH 上取得更低回撤与更优风险调整收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币波段交易存在时间尺度错配——持仓持续期间，市场与事件风险可能在数分钟内变化。而现有预测模型与基于 LLM 的交易系统通常在策略生成之后才评估风险，或让两个功能以相同节奏运行，导致策略提案未以当前风险为条件、且两次修订之间的敞口治理薄弱。

Method: SwingGuard 采用双时间尺度、仅做多的多智能体架构。策略尺度上，多位专业分析师通过看涨—看跌辩论，合成时点（point-in-time）的技术面、情绪面、链上与宏观证据；由 Portfolio Architect 将证据与当前风险上下文结合，形成目标仓位。治理尺度上，5 分钟频率的 Risk Controller 执行条件诊断、维护带分阶段恢复的持续性风险状态、审计提案，并在下一次策略修订前约束执行。

Result: 在比特币（BTC）与以太坊（ETH）上的评估显示，SwingGuard 在扣除交易成本后仍保持正收益，同时维持较浅回撤与较强的风险调整表现。带敞口上限的联合组合夏普比率为 2.98，而其他条件相同的“事后风险”（Post-hoc Risk）变体为 2.79。结构化轨迹将风险证据与状态转移同策略约束及实际执行的仓位变化相连接，形成可审计的异步加密货币波段交易框架。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将策略形成置于当前风险条件下，并配合持续的高频治理，可在不牺牲可审计性的前提下改善风险调整后表现。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency swing trading spans mismatched timescales: positions remain active while market and event risk can change within minutes. However, many predictive and large language model (LLM)-based trading systems assess risk after strategy generation or run both functions at the same cadence, leaving proposals unconditioned by current risk and exposure weakly governed between revisions. We propose SwingGuard, a dual-timescale, long-only multi-agent framework for risk-conditioned strategy generation. At the strategy timescale, specialized analysts synthesize point-in-time technical, sentiment, on-chain, and macroeconomic evidence through bullish-bearish deliberation. The resulting evidence and current risk context are then combined by a Portfolio Architect to form a target position. At the governance timescale, a five-minute Risk Controller performs conditional diagnosis, maintains persistent risk states with staged recovery, audits the proposal, and constrains execution until the next strategy revision. This design allows risk to shape the feasible strategy before proposal finalization while continuing to govern the active position afterward. Evaluations on Bitcoin (BTC) and Ether (ETH) show that SwingGuard preserves positive returns after transaction costs while maintaining shallow drawdowns and strong risk-adjusted performance on both assets. The exposure-capped joint portfolio achieves a Sharpe ratio of 2.98, compared with 2.79 for an otherwise identical Post-hoc Risk variant. Structured traces further link risk evidence and state transitions to strategy constraints and executed position changes, providing an auditable framework for asynchronous cryptocurrency swing trading. Overall, the results indicate that conditioning strategy formation on current risk, together with persistent high-frequency governance, improves risk-adjusted performance without sacrificing auditability.

</details>


### [164] [The Limits of Manipulation within Personalized Incentive Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13133)
*Andrea Araldo,Alessa Mayer,Ravi Seshadri,Amir Brudner*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过Stackelberg博弈框架，建模监管者基于历史行为学习代理画像的过程，分析个性化激励政策中被代理人操纵的条件，发现分类不确定性可抑制操纵并给出操纵收益上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化激励政策将补贴 tailor 到代理人需求与约束，但个性化也使监管者面临代理人策略性操纵的风险；此前对代理人能否通过操纵获利、获利程度如何缺乏理解，限制了此类政策的适用性。

Method: 将问题形式化为Stackelberg博弈：代理人可模拟不同画像以获取更高激励。与既有工作假设监管者即时将代理人归入被模拟画像不同，本文显式建模监管者基于观测到的过去行为学习代理人画像的过程，从而引入被忽视的"操纵成本"（代理人须持续按模拟画像行为而放弃真实偏好）。

Result: 推导出代理人操纵获利的解析条件，并建立操纵收益的上界；该上界随画像对监管者更难区分而更严格。在London Passenger Mode Choice数据集上的仿真验证了这种威慑效应：分类不确定性虽降低个性化精度，却可通过抑制操纵使监管者受益。

Conclusion: 该框架可用于在部署前对个性化激励政策的鲁棒性进行压力测试。反直觉结论是分类不确定性可能因威慑操纵而对监管者有利。

Abstract: Demand management policies aim to shift individual behavior toward sustainable choices (e.g., goods, transport modes). Personalized incentive policies tailor subsidies to agents' needs and constraints, but personalization also exposes the regulator to agents' strategic manipulative behavior. The extent to which agents can profitably manipulate such policies remains poorly understood, which limits their applicability.
  This paper establishes analytically the conditions under which manipulation benefits the agent. We formalize a Stackelberg game in which an agent may simulate a different profile to extract higher incentives from a regulator. Previous work typically assumes that the regulator would instantaneously classify agents into the simulated user profile. In contrast, we explicitly model the regulator's process of learning the agent profile, based on observed past behavior. This makes manipulation harder, since it forces the agent to consistently behave according to a simulated profile, thus renouncing following their true preferences. Manipulative agents thus incur a Cost of Manipulation, which has typically been neglected. By accounting for it, we get a counterintuitive finding: although classification uncertainty reduces personalization accuracy, it may benefit the regulator by deterring manipulation. We establish an upper bound on the agent's gain from manipulation, which becomes stricter as profiles become harder to distinguish for the regulator. Simulations on the London Passenger Mode Choice dataset illustrate this deterrence effect. The framework can therefore be used to stress-test the robustness of personalized incentive policies before deployment.

</details>


### [165] [When AI Advisors Agree: Distinguishing Forecast Risk from Forecast Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13431)
*Russlan Ramdowar*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 七个AI顾问的"一致"并非可靠性的通行证：低分歧组合的平均绝对收益误差更低（10.31 vs 21.05个百分点），但零变动基准预测也呈现几乎相同改善，且方向一致与幅度精确性存在分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是检验"多个AI顾问预测趋同是否意味着更可信"这一直觉。作者指出一致性存在两种解释：一是共享了有用证据，二是该资产本身更易预测；论文旨在区分这两种机制，避免把资产风险、方向共识与幅度精度混同为单一置信度分数。

Method: 使用iPulse AI四个生产批次的928条已完成首季度预测，覆盖232项资产。将十个历史Gemini配置归为七个投资框架，以其存档预测与后续市场结果比对，不生成新报告。分析内容包括：按分歧度分位数比较平均绝对收益误差，设置"无价格变动"基准预测作为对照，控制显性资产风险，并按历史波动率筛选子样本（对比保留低波动季度与保留低分歧季度），以及考察方向一致性（七框架中≥6个同向）与幅度误差的关系。

Result: 最低分歧四分位的平均绝对收益误差为10.31个百分点，最高分歧四分位升至21.05；但零变动基准预测呈现几乎相同的增幅（9.89→21.15），说明分歧与额外误差的关系在控制显性资产风险后仍未厘清。按历史波动率保留最低四分之一预测，误差为7.97个百分点，优于按低分歧保留的10.31。方向层面，当七框架中至少六个方向一致时，方向准确率更高，但收益幅度误差反而更大。

Conclusion: 研究结论是应区分三个常被合并为单一置信度的问题：资产本身的风险水平、顾问对方向是否一致、以及收益幅度预测的精确度。对该历史顾问团而言，一致性只在特定维度具备信息价值，并不构成预测可靠性的普遍保证。

Abstract: If seven AI advisors give similar forecasts, should we trust them more? Agreement can indicate useful shared evidence, but it can also reflect an asset that was easier to forecast in the first place. We examine this distinction using 928 completed first-quarter forecasts for 232 assets across four production batches of iPulse AI. Ten historical Gemini configurations are grouped into seven investment frameworks; their archived forecasts are compared with later market outcomes, without generating new reports. Mean absolute return error rises from 10.31 percentage points in the lowest-disagreement quartile to 21.05 in the highest. Yet a forecast of no price change shows almost the same increase, from 9.89 to 21.15. The relationship between disagreement and additional forecast error is not resolved after accounting for visible asset risk. Retaining the quarter of forecasts with lowest historical volatility produces lower error than retaining those with least disagreement: 7.97 versus 10.31 percentage points. Agreement about direction behaves differently. When at least six of seven frameworks share a direction, directional accuracy is higher, but return-magnitude error is also larger. These results distinguish three questions often collapsed into one confidence score: how risky the asset is, whether advisors agree on its direction, and how precisely they forecast its return. For this historical council, agreement is informative in specific ways; it is not a general certificate of forecast reliability.

</details>


### [166] [Collective Autonomous Vehicle Routing Needs Social Awareness for Coexistence with Human Drivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13439)
*Anastasia Psarou,Mikolaj Rams,Blazej Torbus,Rafal Kucharski*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 在混合自动驾驶交通中，将边际成本这一社会性成分加入AV路径选择的奖励，可缓解AV自私路由对HDV造成的外部性，既降低他人出行时间又改善整体系统表现，且AV自身也受益；仅对影响最大的AV计算边际成本可在显著降低计算成本的同时取得相近效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AV在真实路网中的集体路径选择会产生外部性：对AV有利的路线可能增加其他道路使用者（HDV）的出行时间，因此需要一种超越自私奖励的AV路由目标来兼顾所有交通参与者。

Method: 在MARL框架下研究混合自动驾驶场景中的AV路径选择，AV通过与交通环境反复交互学习路由策略。将社会成分"边际成本"加入自私出行时间奖励中；边际成本通过将与包含该AV的仿真结果对比于移除该AV的反事实仿真中其他使用者的出行时间来度量AV对他人的影响。为提升可扩展性，仅对那些路线选择对交通系统影响最大的AV计算边际成本，从而减少反事实仿真次数。

Result: 在Ingolstadt和Provins真实路网上，加入边际成本不仅限制了HDV出行时间的增加，还缩短了AV自身的出行时间并提升了整体系统性能。以Ingolstadt为例，40% AV的自私路由使其他车辆出行时间增加1.7%，加入社会奖励后降至1.1%；AV在采取社会性行为时反而更受益，该效应在测试城市、渗透率与重复实验中保持一致。但社会成分权重过大时，系统无法达到期望性能。仅对高影响AV计算边际成本可显著降低计算开销并取得相近性能。

Conclusion: AV路由应超越自私奖励，为惠及所有交通参与者而在目标中纳入社会成分；选择性地对高影响AV计算边际成本是实现可扩展社会性AV路由的有效途径，同时需注意社会成分权重不宜过高。

Abstract: Collective Autonomous Vehicle (AV) route choices in real-world traffic networks can create externalities for Human-Driven Vehicles (HDVs), as routes that benefit AVs may increase travel times for other road users. To address this, we introduce a social component, the marginal cost, to the selfish travel time AV reward. Marginal cost captures an AV's impact on other road users by comparing their travel times with those in a counterfactual simulation in which the AV is removed.
  We study AV route choice in mixed-autonomy traffic settings using Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL), where AVs learn routing policies through repeated interactions with the traffic environment. Experiments on the real-world networks of Ingolstadt and Provins show that incorporating marginal cost into the AV reward not only limits the increase in HDV travel times and shortens AV travel times relative to selfish AV routing, but also improves overall system performance. In Ingolstadt, selfish routing of 40\% AVs increases the travel times of other vehicles by 1.7\%, and this drops to 1.1\% when the social reward is included. Interestingly, not only does overall system performance improve, but the AVs are also better off when acting socially.This effect is consistent across tested cities, penetration rates, and replications, yet when the weight of the social component in the reward increases, the system fails to reach the desired performance.
  For a more scalable approach to social AV routing in mixed-autonomy traffic, we compute the marginal cost only for the AVs whose route choices most affect the traffic system. This reduces the number of counterfactual simulations and achieves similar performance at a significantly lower computational cost. Our results suggest that AV routing should go beyond selfish rewards and, for the benefit of all traffic participants, include a social component in its objective.

</details>


### [167] [OpenSocialJax: Evaluating Social Adaptation in Sequential Social Dilemmas with Open-Ended Environmental Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13710)
*Zihao Guo,Meng Fang,Joel Z. Leibo,Yali Du*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出 OpenSocialJax 基准，将 Clean Up 与 Commons Harvest 扩展到千万级潜在环境配置，测试八个大语言模型在规则发现与多智能体适应上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列社会困境基准虽覆盖多样社会互动，却极少检验智能体能否在重复交互中推断隐藏环境规则，并同时适应其他智能体行为。

Method: 构建 OpenSocialJax，对空间布局及工具制作、收益、资源动态等隐藏规则进行开放式配置采样；每回合从数千万种配置中抽取一种并在五次试次中保持不变。评估三个闭源与五个开源大语言模型，在同质与混合团队中考察规则发现与适应能力，重点为 OpenCleanup 与 OpenHarvest 两个任务。

Result: 规则发现通常提升性能，但不能稳定维持合作：OpenCleanup 中清理负担集中于少数智能体；OpenHarvest 中各团队最终均耗尽共享资源。混合竞技场中，一个合作型智能体可在牺牲自身收益的情况下维持 OpenCleanup 的公共物品，但无法阻止 OpenHarvest 的资源枯竭。

Conclusion: 结果表明，智能体难以同时对陌生环境动态与其他智能体行为变化进行联合适应，凸显开放环境中规则推断与社会协调的双重挑战。

Abstract: Sequential social dilemma benchmarks capture diverse social interactions, but rarely test whether agents can infer hidden environmental rules through repeated interaction while adapting to other agents. We introduce \textbf{OpenSocialJax}, which extends Clean Up and Commons Harvest across an open-ended distribution of latent environment configurations, systematically varying spatial layouts and hidden rules governing tool crafting, payoffs, and resource dynamics. Each episode samples one configuration from tens of millions of possibilities and holds it fixed across five trials. We evaluate eight large language models, including three closed source models and five open source models. Our evaluation tests whether models can discover hidden rules and adapt to other agents in homogeneous and mixed teams. We find that rule discovery often improves performance but does not reliably sustain cooperation, as the cleaning burden becomes concentrated among fewer agents in OpenCleanup and every team eventually exhausts the shared resource in OpenHarvest. In our mixed arena, one cooperative agent was able to sustain the public good in OpenCleanup at a cost to its own return, but could not prevent resource depletion in OpenHarvest. Together, these results highlight the difficulty of adapting jointly to unfamiliar environmental dynamics and the changing behavior of other agents.

</details>


### [168] [Ask Your Advisor: Learning When to Consult Stronger Models in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13870)
*Pascal Sowietzki,Steffen Holter,Yannick Metz,Emil Kasper,Raphael Baur,Mennatallah El-Assady*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: ARIA 是一个学习"何时咨询更强模型"的元控制器，将咨询决策建模为序列资源分配问题，在 SWE-bench Verified、Terminal-Bench 2.1 与 τ²-bench 上以不到一半的顾问成本恢复 44-81% 的执行者-顾问成功率差距，取得最优的成功-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行者-顾问（executor-advisor）智能体工作流为平衡性能与算力开销，采用低成本模型执行、并在需要时选择性咨询强模型。但当前语言模型难以自我识别推理失败之处，且对强推理的需求在执行过程中动态出现、随工作流阶段变化，因此需要一种能够自主判断何时升级咨询的机制。

Method: 提出 ARIA 元控制器，将"是否咨询"形式化为序列资源分配问题：在工作流的接触点（contact points）上，将执行证据映射为紧凑的状态抽象，并选择 SILENT 或 CONSULT 动作；动作价值通过 Monte Carlo 控制，基于带成本惩罚的结果进行学习。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified、Terminal-Bench 2.1 和 τ²-bench 上，ARIA 以不足顾问模型一半的成本恢复了 44-81% 的执行者-顾问成功率差距，在十种对照条件（路由、级联、固定与提示式咨询、等咨询量下的随机放置）中取得最佳的成功-成本权衡。

Conclusion: ARIA 学到的策略紧凑且可审查，仅通过单一"咨询价格"参数即可调节成功-成本权衡，且无需重新训练任一底层模型。

Abstract: To balance task performance and computational cost, agentic workflows can use a lower-cost model for execution and selectively consult a stronger model. In such executor-advisor systems, the executor decides when to seek advice, making escalation depend on self-assessment, although current language models struggle to detect where their own reasoning fails. The need for stronger reasoning emerges during execution and varies across workflow phases. We introduce ARIA, a meta-controller that learns when to consult by balancing task success against consultation cost. Our approach formulates consultation as a sequential resource-allocation problem. At workflow contact points, ARIA maps execution evidence to a compact state abstraction and selects SILENT or CONSULT, with action values learned by Monte Carlo control from cost-penalized outcomes. Across evaluated tasks of SWE-bench Verified, Terminal-Bench 2.1, and $τ^2$-bench, ARIA recovers 44-81% of the executor-advisor success gap at less than half the advisor model's cost. ARIA achieves the best observed success-cost trade-off among ten other evaluated conditions, including routing and cascading methods, fixed and prompted consultation, and random placement at matched advice volume. The policies are compact and inspectable, with a single consultation-price parameter governing the success-cost trade-off without retraining either underlying model.

</details>
